using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ER.Core.Localization
{
    [Serializable]
    public class LocalizationEntry
    {
        public string key;

        public string value;
    }

    [Serializable]
    public class LocalizationPackage
    {
        public string languageCode;

        public string languageDisplayName;

        public List<LocalizationEntry> entries = new List<LocalizationEntry>();
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\01_Core\Localization\LocalizationManager.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.Core.Localization
{
    public class LocalizationManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static LocalizationManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Active Language")]
        [SerializeField] private string currentLanguageCode = "ru_RU";

        [SerializeField] private string fallbackLanguageCode = "en_US";


        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> activeLanguageDictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();

        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> fallbackLanguageDictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();

        private readonly List<string> availableLanguages = new List<string>();

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public event Action OnLanguageChanged;


        public string CurrentLanguageCode => currentLanguageCode;

        public IReadOnlyList<string> AvailableLanguages => availableLanguages;

        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);

            InitializeLocalizationSystem();
        }


        public void InitializeLocalizationSystem()
        {
            ScanAvailableLanguages();

            LoadFallbackDictionary();

            LoadActiveDictionary(currentLanguageCode);

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private string GetLocalizationDirectoryPath()
        {
            string path = Path.Combine(Application.streamingAssetsPath, "Localization");

            if (!Directory.Exists(path))
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(path);
            }

            return path;
        }


        private void ScanAvailableLanguages()
        {
            availableLanguages.Clear();

            string dirPath = GetLocalizationDirectoryPath();

            string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(dirPath, "*.json");

            for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
            {
                string languageCode = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(files[i]);

                availableLanguages.Add(languageCode);
            }

            if (!availableLanguages.Contains("en_US"))
            {
                CreateDefaultEnglishPackage();

                availableLanguages.Add("en_US");
            }

            if (!availableLanguages.Contains("ru_RU"))
            {
                CreateDefaultRussianPackage();

                availableLanguages.Add("ru_RU");
            }
        }


        private void LoadFallbackDictionary()
        {
            fallbackLanguageDictionary.Clear();

            string filePath = Path.Combine(GetLocalizationDirectoryPath(), $"{fallbackLanguageCode}.json");

            if (!File.Exists(filePath))
            {
                return;
            }

            string jsonContent = File.ReadAllText(filePath);

            LocalizationPackage package = JsonUtility.FromJson<LocalizationPackage>(jsonContent);

            if (package != null && package.entries != null)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < package.entries.Count; i++)
                {
                    LocalizationEntry entry = package.entries[i];

                    fallbackLanguageDictionary[entry.key] = entry.value;
                }
            }
        }


        private void LoadActiveDictionary(string languageCode)
        {
            activeLanguageDictionary.Clear();

            string filePath = Path.Combine(GetLocalizationDirectoryPath(), $"{languageCode}.json");

            if (File.Exists(filePath))
            {
                string jsonContent = File.ReadAllText(filePath);

                LocalizationPackage package = JsonUtility.FromJson<LocalizationPackage>(jsonContent);

                if (package != null && package.entries != null)
                {
                    for (int i = 0; i < package.entries.Count; i++)
                    {
                        LocalizationEntry entry = package.entries[i];

                        activeLanguageDictionary[entry.key] = entry.value;
                    }
                }

                currentLanguageCode = languageCode;
            }
            else
            {
                currentLanguageCode = fallbackLanguageCode;
            }

            OnLanguageChanged?.Invoke();
        }


        public void SetLanguage(string languageCode)
        {
            if (currentLanguageCode == languageCode && activeLanguageDictionary.Count > 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            LoadActiveDictionary(languageCode);
        }


        public string GetText(string key)
        {
            if (activeLanguageDictionary.TryGetValue(key, out string localizedValue))
            {
                return localizedValue;
            }

            if (fallbackLanguageDictionary.TryGetValue(key, out string fallbackValue))
            {
                return fallbackValue;
            }

            return $"MISSING_{key}";
        }


        public string GetTextFormat(string key, params object[] args)
        {
            string rawPattern = GetText(key);

            try
            {
                return string.Format(rawPattern, args);
            }
            catch
            {
                return rawPattern;
            }
        }


        private void CreateDefaultEnglishPackage()
        {
            LocalizationPackage package = new LocalizationPackage
            {
                languageCode = "en_US",
                languageDisplayName = "English"
            };

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.spring", value = "Spring" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.summer", value = "Summer" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.autumn", value = "Autumn" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.winter", value = "Winter" });

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.plains", value = "Plains" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.hills", value = "Hills" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.mountains", value = "Mountains" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.water_shallow", value = "Shallow Water" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.water_deep", value = "Deep Ocean" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.swamp", value = "Swamp" });

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "ui.date_format", value = "Year {0}, {1}, Day {2:00}, {3:00}:00" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "ui.pause", value = "PAUSED" });

            SavePackageToFile("en_US", package);
        }


        private void CreateDefaultRussianPackage()
        {
            LocalizationPackage package = new LocalizationPackage
            {
                languageCode = "ru_RU",
                languageDisplayName = "Р СѓСЃСЃРєРёР№"
            };

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.spring", value = "Р’РµСЃРЅР°" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.summer", value = "Р›РµС‚Рѕ" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.autumn", value = "РћСЃРµРЅСЊ" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "season.winter", value = "Р—РёРјР°" });

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.plains", value = "Р Р°РІРЅРёРЅС‹" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.hills", value = "РҐРѕР»РјС‹" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.mountains", value = "Р“РѕСЂС‹" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.water_shallow", value = "РњРµР»РєРѕРІРѕРґСЊРµ / Р РµРєР°" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.water_deep", value = "Р“Р»СѓР±РѕРєРёРµ Р’РѕРґС‹" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "terrain.swamp", value = "Р‘РѕР»РѕС‚Рѕ" });

            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "ui.date_format", value = "Р“РѕРґ {0}, {1}, Р”РµРЅСЊ {2:00}, {3:00}:00" });
            package.entries.Add(new LocalizationEntry { key = "ui.pause", value = "РџРђРЈР—Рђ" });

            SavePackageToFile("ru_RU", package);
        }


        private void SavePackageToFile(string languageCode, LocalizationPackage package)
        {
            string filePath = Path.Combine(GetLocalizationDirectoryPath(), $"{languageCode}.json");

            string json = JsonUtility.ToJson(package, true);

            File.WriteAllText(filePath, json);
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\01_Core\UI\LocalizedText.cs ===
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.Core.Localization;

namespace ER.Core.UI
{
    [RequireComponent(typeof(TextMeshProUGUI))]
    public class LocalizedText : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("Localization Key")]
        [SerializeField] private string localizationKey;


        private TextMeshProUGUI textComponent;


        public string LocalizationKey => localizationKey;


        private void Awake()
        {
            textComponent = GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
        }


        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LocalizationManager.Instance.OnLanguageChanged += RefreshText;
            }

            RefreshText();
        }


        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LocalizationManager.Instance.OnLanguageChanged -= RefreshText;
            }
        }


        public void SetKey(string newKey)
        {
            localizationKey = newKey;

            RefreshText();
        }


        public void RefreshText()
        {
            if (textComponent == null)
            {
                textComponent = GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
            }

            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(localizationKey))
            {
                textComponent.text = LocalizationManager.Instance.GetText(localizationKey);
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\01_Core\UI\TopTimeBarUI.cs ===
using System.Text;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using ER.Simulation.Time;
using ER.Core.Localization;

namespace ER.Core.UI
{
    public class TopTimeBarUI : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("UI Text Displays")]
        [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI dateDisplayLabel;

        [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI speedStatusLabel;

        [SerializeField] private TextMeshProUGUI tickIndexLabel;


        [Header("Controls")]
        [SerializeField] private Button pauseButton;

        [SerializeField] private Button speed1Button;

        [SerializeField] private Button speed2Button;

        [SerializeField] private Button speed3Button;

        [SerializeField] private Button speed4Button;

        [SerializeField] private Button speed5Button;


        [Header("References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationTimeConfig timeConfig;


        private readonly StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(128);


        private void Start()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;
            }

            BindButtonEvents();

            SubscribeToSimulationEvents();

            UpdateFullDisplay();
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            UnsubscribeFromSimulationEvents();
        }


        private void BindButtonEvents()
        {
            if (pauseButton != null) pauseButton.onClick.AddListener(OnPauseClicked);

            if (speed1Button != null) speed1Button.onClick.AddListener(() => OnSpeedClicked(timeConfig.Speed1));

            if (speed2Button != null) speed2Button.onClick.AddListener(() => OnSpeedClicked(timeConfig.Speed2));

            if (speed3Button != null) speed3Button.onClick.AddListener(() => OnSpeedClicked(timeConfig.Speed3));

            if (speed4Button != null) speed4Button.onClick.AddListener(() => OnSpeedClicked(timeConfig.Speed4));

            if (speed5Button != null) speed5Button.onClick.AddListener(() => OnSpeedClicked(timeConfig.Speed5));
        }


        private void SubscribeToSimulationEvents()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            clockManager.OnTick += HandleTick;

            clockManager.OnDayPassed += HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnSeasonChanged += HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnYearPassed += HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnPauseStateChanged += HandlePauseStateChanged;

            clockManager.OnSpeedMultiplierChanged += HandleSpeedChanged;

            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LocalizationManager.Instance.OnLanguageChanged += UpdateFullDisplay;
            }
        }


        private void UnsubscribeFromSimulationEvents()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            clockManager.OnTick -= HandleTick;

            clockManager.OnDayPassed -= HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnSeasonChanged -= HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnYearPassed -= HandleDateChange;

            clockManager.OnPauseStateChanged -= HandlePauseStateChanged;

            clockManager.OnSpeedMultiplierChanged -= HandleSpeedChanged;

            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null)
            {
                LocalizationManager.Instance.OnLanguageChanged -= UpdateFullDisplay;
            }
        }


        private void HandleTick(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            UpdateDateText(timeData);

            UpdateTickIndexText(timeData.TotalTicks);
        }


        private void HandleDateChange(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            UpdateDateText(timeData);
        }


        private void HandlePauseStateChanged(bool isPaused)
        {
            UpdateSpeedStatusDisplay();
        }


        private void HandleSpeedChanged(float speed)
        {
            UpdateSpeedStatusDisplay();
        }


        private void UpdateFullDisplay()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            WorldTimeData currentTime = clockManager.CurrentTime;

            UpdateDateText(currentTime);

            UpdateTickIndexText(currentTime.TotalTicks);

            UpdateSpeedStatusDisplay();
        }


        private void UpdateDateText(WorldTimeData time)
        {
            if (dateDisplayLabel == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            string seasonKey = GetSeasonLocalizationKey(time.CurrentSeason);

            string seasonName = LocalizationManager.Instance != null ? LocalizationManager.Instance.GetText(seasonKey) : time.CurrentSeason.ToString();

            stringBuilder.Clear();

            if (LocalizationManager.Instance != null)
            {
                string formatPattern = LocalizationManager.Instance.GetText("ui.date_format");

                stringBuilder.AppendFormat(formatPattern, time.CurrentYear, seasonName, time.CurrentDay, time.CurrentHour);
            }
            else
            {
                stringBuilder.Append($"Year {time.CurrentYear}, {seasonName}, Day {time.CurrentDay:00}, {time.CurrentHour:00}:00");
            }

            dateDisplayLabel.text = stringBuilder.ToString();
        }


        private void UpdateTickIndexText(ulong ticks)
        {
            if (tickIndexLabel == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            stringBuilder.Clear();

            stringBuilder.Append("Tick #").Append(ticks);

            tickIndexLabel.text = stringBuilder.ToString();
        }


        private void UpdateSpeedStatusDisplay()
        {
            if (speedStatusLabel == null || clockManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            stringBuilder.Clear();

            if (clockManager.IsPaused)
            {
                string pauseWord = LocalizationManager.Instance != null ? LocalizationManager.Instance.GetText("ui.pause") : "PAUSED";

                stringBuilder.Append(pauseWord);
            }
            else
            {
                stringBuilder.Append(clockManager.CurrentSpeedMultiplier.ToString("0.#")).Append("x");
            }

            speedStatusLabel.text = stringBuilder.ToString();
        }


        private string GetSeasonLocalizationKey(Season season)
        {
            switch (season)
            {
                case Season.Spring:
                    return "season.spring";

                case Season.Summer:
                    return "season.summer";

                case Season.Autumn:
                    return "season.autumn";

                case Season.Winter:
                    return "season.winter";

                default:
                    return "season.spring";
            }
        }


        private void OnPauseClicked()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.TogglePause();
            }
        }


        private void OnSpeedClicked(float speedMultiplier)
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.SetPaused(false);

                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(speedMultiplier);
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\02_Simulation\Time\SimulationClockManager.cs ===
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.Simulation.Time
{
    public class SimulationClockManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static SimulationClockManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration Asset")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationTimeConfig timeConfig;


        [Header("Runtime State")]
        [SerializeField] private bool isPaused = false;

        [SerializeField] private float currentSpeedMultiplier = 1.0f;


        private WorldTimeData currentTimeData;

        private float timeAccumulator;

        private readonly Stopwatch tickStopwatch = new Stopwatch();

        private double lastTickExecutionDurationMs;


        public event Action<WorldTimeData> OnTick;

        public event Action<WorldTimeData> OnDayPassed;

        public event Action<WorldTimeData> OnSeasonChanged;

        public event Action<WorldTimeData> OnYearPassed;

        public event Action<bool> OnPauseStateChanged;

        public event Action<float> OnSpeedMultiplierChanged;


        public WorldTimeData CurrentTime => currentTimeData;

        public bool IsPaused => isPaused;

        public float CurrentSpeedMultiplier => currentSpeedMultiplier;

        public double LastTickExecutionDurationMs => lastTickExecutionDurationMs;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            currentTimeData = WorldTimeData.CreateInitial();
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (isPaused || timeConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            float frameDelta = Mathf.Min(UnityEngine.Time.unscaledDeltaTime, timeConfig.MaxFrameAccumulationTime);

            timeAccumulator += frameDelta * currentSpeedMultiplier;

            float targetTickDuration = timeConfig.BaseSecondsPerTick;

            while (timeAccumulator >= targetTickDuration)
            {
                ExecuteSimulationTick();

                timeAccumulator -= targetTickDuration;
            }
        }


        private void ExecuteSimulationTick()
        {
            tickStopwatch.Restart();

            currentTimeData.AdvanceTick(out bool dayChanged, out bool seasonChanged, out bool yearChanged);

            OnTick?.Invoke(currentTimeData);

            if (dayChanged)
            {
                OnDayPassed?.Invoke(currentTimeData);
            }

            if (seasonChanged)
            {
                OnSeasonChanged?.Invoke(currentTimeData);
            }

            if (yearChanged)
            {
                OnYearPassed?.Invoke(currentTimeData);
            }

            tickStopwatch.Stop();

            lastTickExecutionDurationMs = tickStopwatch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
        }


        public void SetPaused(bool paused)
        {
            if (isPaused == paused)
            {
                return;
            }

            isPaused = paused;

            timeAccumulator = 0.0f;

            OnPauseStateChanged?.Invoke(isPaused);
        }


        public void TogglePause()
        {
            SetPaused(!isPaused);
        }


        public void SetSpeedMultiplier(float multiplier)
        {
            if (Mathf.Approximately(currentSpeedMultiplier, multiplier) || multiplier <= 0.0f)
            {
                return;
            }

            currentSpeedMultiplier = multiplier;

            OnSpeedMultiplierChanged?.Invoke(currentSpeedMultiplier);
        }


        public void SetLoadedTime(WorldTimeData loadedTime)
        {
            currentTimeData = loadedTime;

            timeAccumulator = 0.0f;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\02_Simulation\Time\SimulationHotkeysDebugger.cs ===
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.Simulation.Time
{
    public class SimulationHotkeysDebugger : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationTimeConfig timeConfig;


        [Header("Debug Overlay State")]
        [SerializeField] private bool showDebugOverlay = false;


        private float fpsAccumulator = 0.0f;

        private int fpsFramesCount = 0;

        private float currentFps = 0.0f;

        private float currentFrameTimeMs = 0.0f;

        private float fpsUpdateTimer = 0.0f;

        private const float FpsUpdateInterval = 0.25f;


        public bool ShowDebugOverlay => showDebugOverlay;


        private void Start()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;
            }
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F3))
            {
                showDebugOverlay = !showDebugOverlay;
            }

            if (showDebugOverlay)
            {
                CalculatePerformanceMetrics();
            }

            HandleSimulationInput();
        }


        private void CalculatePerformanceMetrics()
        {
            float delta = UnityEngine.Time.unscaledDeltaTime;

            fpsAccumulator += delta;

            fpsFramesCount++;

            fpsUpdateTimer += delta;

            if (fpsUpdateTimer >= FpsUpdateInterval)
            {
                currentFps = fpsFramesCount / fpsAccumulator;

                currentFrameTimeMs = (fpsAccumulator / fpsFramesCount) * 1000.0f;

                fpsAccumulator = 0.0f;

                fpsFramesCount = 0;

                fpsUpdateTimer = 0.0f;
            }
        }


        private void HandleSimulationInput()
        {
            if (clockManager == null || timeConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Space))
            {
                clockManager.TogglePause();
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha1))
            {
                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(timeConfig.Speed1);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha2))
            {
                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(timeConfig.Speed2);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha3))
            {
                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(timeConfig.Speed3);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha4))
            {
                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(timeConfig.Speed4);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha5))
            {
                clockManager.SetSpeedMultiplier(timeConfig.Speed5);
            }
        }


        private void OnGUI()
        {
            if (!showDebugOverlay || clockManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            WorldTimeData time = clockManager.CurrentTime;

            string statusText = clockManager.IsPaused ? "PAUSED" : $"{clockManager.CurrentSpeedMultiplier:0.#}x";

            GUI.Box(new Rect(12, 60, 540, 115), "Simulation Engine Telemetry (F3 to Hide)");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 82, 510, 20), $"Status: {statusText}  |  Controls: [Space] Pause  |  [1..5] Speeds");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 102, 510, 20), $"Date: Year {time.CurrentYear}, {time.CurrentSeason}, Day {time.CurrentDay:00}, Hour {time.CurrentHour:00}:00");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 122, 510, 20), $"Tick: #{time.TotalTicks}  |  Sim Tick: {clockManager.LastTickExecutionDurationMs:F4} ms");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 142, 510, 20), $"Render: {currentFps:0.} FPS ({currentFrameTimeMs:F2} ms/frame)");
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\02_Simulation\Time\SimulationTimeConfig.cs ===
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.Simulation.Time
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "SimulationTimeConfig", menuName = "ER/02_Simulation/Time/Time Config")]
    public class SimulationTimeConfig : ScriptableObject
    {
        [Header("Base Tick Duration")]
        [SerializeField] private float baseSecondsPerTick = 2.5f;
        [SerializeField] private float maxFrameAccumulationTime = 1.0f;

        [Header("Speed Multipliers")]
        [SerializeField] private float speed1 = 1.0f;
        [SerializeField] private float speed2 = 2.0f;

        public float BaseSecondsPerTick => baseSecondsPerTick;
        public float MaxFrameAccumulationTime => maxFrameAccumulationTime;

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\02_Simulation\Time\WorldTimeData.cs ===
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.Simulation.Time
{
    public enum Season : byte
    {
        Spring = 0,
        Summer = 1,
        Autumn = 2,
        Winter = 3
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct WorldTimeData
    {
        public const int TicksPerHour = 50;

        public const int HoursPerDay = 24;

        public const int DaysPerSeason = 16;

        public const int SeasonsPerYear = 4;

        public const int TicksPerDay = HoursPerDay * TicksPerHour;

        public const int TicksPerSeason = DaysPerSeason * TicksPerDay;

        public const int TicksPerYear = SeasonsPerYear * TicksPerSeason;

        public const int DaysPerYear = DaysPerSeason * SeasonsPerYear;


        public ulong TotalTicks;

        public int CurrentHour;

        public int CurrentDay;

        public int CurrentYear;

        public Season CurrentSeason;

        public float AmbientTemperature;


        public bool IsFreezing => AmbientTemperature <= 0.0f;

        public float DailyProgressRatio => (TotalTicks % TicksPerDay) / (float)TicksPerDay;


        public static WorldTimeData CreateInitial()
        {
            WorldTimeData time = new WorldTimeData
            {
                TotalTicks = 0,
                CurrentHour = 0,
                CurrentDay = 1,
                CurrentSeason = Season.Spring,
                CurrentYear = 1,
                AmbientTemperature = CalculateAmbientTemperature(Season.Spring, 1, 0, 0)
            };

            return time;
        }


        public void AdvanceTick(out bool dayChanged, out bool seasonChanged, out bool yearChanged)
        {
            TotalTicks++;

            dayChanged = false;

            seasonChanged = false;

            yearChanged = false;

            int tickInDay = (int)(TotalTicks % (ulong)TicksPerDay);

            CurrentHour = tickInDay / TicksPerHour;

            if (tickInDay == 0)
            {
                dayChanged = true;

                CurrentDay++;

                if (CurrentDay > DaysPerSeason)
                {
                    CurrentDay = 1;

                    seasonChanged = true;

                    byte nextSeason = (byte)((byte)CurrentSeason + 1);

                    if (nextSeason >= SeasonsPerYear)
                    {
                        CurrentSeason = Season.Spring;

                        CurrentYear++;

                        yearChanged = true;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        CurrentSeason = (Season)nextSeason;
                    }
                }
            }

            AmbientTemperature = CalculateAmbientTemperature(CurrentSeason, CurrentDay, CurrentHour, tickInDay);
        }


        public static float CalculateAmbientTemperature(Season season, int dayInSeason, int hour, int tickInDay)
        {
            float dayProgress = (tickInDay % TicksPerDay) / (float)TicksPerDay;

            float totalYearDay = ((int)season * DaysPerSeason) + (dayInSeason - 1) + dayProgress;

            float summerPeakDay = DaysPerSeason * 1.5f;

            float seasonAngle = (totalYearDay - summerPeakDay) * (2.0f * Mathf.PI / DaysPerYear);

            float seasonalTemp = 6.0f + Mathf.Cos(seasonAngle) * 16.0f;

            float hourProgress = (hour + (tickInDay % TicksPerHour) / (float)TicksPerHour) / (float)HoursPerDay;

            float dayAngle = (hourProgress - 0.58f) * (2.0f * Mathf.PI);

            float diurnalTemp = Mathf.Cos(dayAngle) * 3.5f;

            return seasonalTemp + diurnalTemp;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Audio
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "AdaptiveMusicConfig", menuName = "ER/03_World/Audio/Adaptive Music Config")]
    public class AdaptiveMusicConfig : ScriptableObject
    {
        [Header("Mood Playlists")]
        [SerializeField] private List<AudioClip> peaceTracks = new List<AudioClip>();

        [SerializeField] private List<AudioClip> tensionTracks = new List<AudioClip>();

        [SerializeField] private List<AudioClip> warTracks = new List<AudioClip>();


        [Header("Crossfade and Volume")]
        [SerializeField] [Range(0.0f, 1.0f)] private float masterMusicVolume = 0.60f;

        [SerializeField] private float crossfadeDuration = 3.0f;


        public IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> PeaceTracks => peaceTracks;

        public IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> TensionTracks => tensionTracks;

        public IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> WarTracks => warTracks;

        public float MasterMusicVolume => masterMusicVolume;

        public float CrossfadeDuration => crossfadeDuration;


        public IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> GetPlaylistByMood(MusicMood mood)
        {
            switch (mood)
            {
                case MusicMood.Peace:
                    return peaceTracks;

                case MusicMood.Tension:
                    return tensionTracks;

                case MusicMood.War:
                    return warTracks;

                default:
                    return peaceTracks;
            }
        }
    }

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Audio\AdaptiveMusicManager.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Audio
{
    public class AdaptiveMusicManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static AdaptiveMusicManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration Asset")]
        [SerializeField] private AdaptiveMusicConfig musicConfig;


        [Header("Dynamic State")]
        [SerializeField] private MusicMood currentMood = MusicMood.Peace;

        [SerializeField] [Range(0.0f, 1.0f)] private float tensionLevel = 0.0f;

        [SerializeField] [Range(0.0f, 1.0f)] private float warThreatLevel = 0.0f;


        private AudioSource sourceA;

        private AudioSource sourceB;

        private bool isSourceAActive = true;


        private float crossfadeTimer = 0.0f;

        private bool isCrossfading = false;


        private int lastPlayedClipIndex = -1;

        private AudioClip activeClip;


        public MusicMood CurrentMood => currentMood;

        public float TensionLevel => tensionLevel;

        public float WarThreatLevel => warThreatLevel;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeDualChannels();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EvaluateMoodState();

            PlayNextTrack(true);
        }


        private void InitializeDualChannels()
        {
            sourceA = CreateAudioChannel("MusicChannel_A");

            sourceB = CreateAudioChannel("MusicChannel_B");
        }


        private AudioSource CreateAudioChannel(string channelName)
        {
            GameObject channelObj = new GameObject(channelName);

            channelObj.transform.SetParent(transform);

            AudioSource source = channelObj.AddComponent<AudioSource>();

            source.loop = false;

            source.playOnAwake = false;

            source.volume = 0.0f;

            source.spatialBlend = 0.0f;

            return source;
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (musicConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            HandleMoodEvaluation();

            HandleCrossfadeProgression();

            HandleTrackEndDetection();
        }


        private void HandleMoodEvaluation()
        {
            MusicMood evaluatedMood = EvaluateMoodState();

            if (evaluatedMood != currentMood)
            {
                currentMood = evaluatedMood;

                PlayNextTrack(false);
            }
        }


        private MusicMood EvaluateMoodState()
        {
            if (warThreatLevel >= 0.5f)
            {
                return MusicMood.War;
            }

            if (tensionLevel >= 0.4f)
            {
                return MusicMood.Tension;
            }

            return MusicMood.Peace;
        }


        private void HandleCrossfadeProgression()
        {
            if (!isCrossfading)
            {
                return;
            }

            crossfadeTimer += UnityEngine.Time.unscaledDeltaTime;

            float progress = Mathf.Clamp01(crossfadeTimer / Mathf.Max(musicConfig.CrossfadeDuration, 0.1f));

            float master = musicConfig.MasterMusicVolume;

            AudioSource activeSource = isSourceAActive ? sourceA : sourceB;

            AudioSource fadingSource = isSourceAActive ? sourceB : sourceA;

            activeSource.volume = progress * master;

            fadingSource.volume = (1.0f - progress) * master;

            if (progress >= 1.0f)
            {
                fadingSource.Stop();

                fadingSource.clip = null;

                isCrossfading = false;

                crossfadeTimer = 0.0f;
            }
        }


        private void HandleTrackEndDetection()
        {
            if (isCrossfading)
            {
                return;
            }

            AudioSource activeSource = isSourceAActive ? sourceA : sourceB;

            if (activeSource.clip != null && !activeSource.isPlaying && activeSource.time >= (activeSource.clip.length - 0.2f))
            {
                PlayNextTrack(false);
            }
        }


        public void PlayNextTrack(bool immediate)
        {
            if (musicConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> playlist = musicConfig.GetPlaylistByMood(currentMood);

            if (playlist == null || playlist.Count == 0)
            {
                playlist = musicConfig.PeaceTracks;
            }

            if (playlist == null || playlist.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            AudioClip nextClip = SelectNextClip(playlist);

            if (nextClip == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (immediate)
            {
                sourceA.Stop();

                sourceB.Stop();

                sourceA.clip = nextClip;

                sourceA.volume = musicConfig.MasterMusicVolume;

                sourceA.Play();

                isSourceAActive = true;

                isCrossfading = false;

                activeClip = nextClip;
            }
            else
            {
                AudioSource incomingSource = isSourceAActive ? sourceB : sourceA;

                incomingSource.Stop();

                incomingSource.clip = nextClip;

                incomingSource.volume = 0.0f;

                incomingSource.Play();

                isSourceAActive = !isSourceAActive;

                isCrossfading = true;

                crossfadeTimer = 0.0f;

                activeClip = nextClip;
            }
        }


        private AudioClip SelectNextClip(IReadOnlyList<AudioClip> playlist)
        {
            if (playlist.Count == 1)
            {
                lastPlayedClipIndex = 0;

                return playlist[0];
            }

            int candidateIndex;

            int attempts = 0;

            do
            {
                candidateIndex = Random.Range(0, playlist.Count);

                attempts++;
            }
            while (candidateIndex == lastPlayedClipIndex && attempts < 10);

            lastPlayedClipIndex = candidateIndex;

            return playlist[candidateIndex];
        }


        public void SetTensionLevel(float value)
        {
            tensionLevel = Mathf.Clamp01(value);
        }


        public void SetWarThreatLevel(float value)
        {
            warThreatLevel = Mathf.Clamp01(value);
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Audio\SpatialSoundscapeManager.cs ===
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.Core.Cameras;
using ER.Simulation.Time;

namespace ER.World.Audio
{
    public class SpatialSoundscapeManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static SpatialSoundscapeManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration")]
        [SerializeField] private SpatialSoundscapeConfig soundConfig;


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private StrategicCameraController cameraController;

        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;


        private AudioSource plainsSource;

        private AudioSource forestSource;

        private AudioSource waterSource;

        private AudioSource windSource;


        private float targetPlainsVolume = 0.0f;

        private float targetForestVolume = 0.0f;

        private float targetWaterVolume = 0.0f;

        private float targetWindVolume = 0.0f;


        private float analysisTimer = 0.0f;

        private const float AnalysisInterval = 0.25f;

        private bool wasWinterActive = false;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeAudioSources();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            if (cameraController == null)
            {
                cameraController = StrategicCameraController.Instance != null 
                    ? StrategicCameraController.Instance 
                    : FindFirstObjectByType<StrategicCameraController>();
            }

            if (gridManager == null)
            {
                gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;
            }

            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;
            }
        }


        private void InitializeAudioSources()
        {
            plainsSource = CreateChannel("Ambience_Plains");

            forestSource = CreateChannel("Ambience_Forest");

            waterSource = CreateChannel("Ambience_Water");

            windSource = CreateChannel("Ambience_Wind");

            if (soundConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            AssignClipToChannel(plainsSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.PlainsVariations));

            AssignClipToChannel(forestSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.ForestVariations));

            AssignClipToChannel(waterSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.WaterVariations));

            AssignClipToChannel(windSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.WindVariations));
        }


        private AudioSource CreateChannel(string channelName)
        {
            GameObject channelObj = new GameObject(channelName);

            channelObj.transform.SetParent(transform);

            AudioSource source = channelObj.AddComponent<AudioSource>();

            source.loop = true;

            source.playOnAwake = false;

            source.volume = 0.0f;

            source.spatialBlend = 0.0f;

            return source;
        }


        private void AssignClipToChannel(AudioSource source, AudioClip clip)
        {
            if (clip != null)
            {
                source.clip = clip;

                source.Play();
            }
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (soundConfig == null || cameraController == null || gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            analysisTimer += UnityEngine.Time.unscaledDeltaTime;

            if (analysisTimer >= AnalysisInterval)
            {
                AnalyzeViewportLandscape();

                analysisTimer = 0.0f;
            }

            InterpolateAudioVolumes();
        }


        private void AnalyzeViewportLandscape()
        {
            Vector3 cameraPosition = cameraController.transform.position;

            WorldMapConfig mapConfig = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int centerTileX = Mathf.RoundToInt(cameraPosition.x / mapConfig.TileWorldSize);

            int centerTileY = Mathf.RoundToInt(cameraPosition.y / mapConfig.TileWorldSize);

            int radius = Mathf.RoundToInt(soundConfig.SampleRadiusInTiles);

            int waterCount = 0;

            int forestCount = 0;

            int plainsCount = 0;

            int mountainCount = 0;

            int totalSamples = 0;

            for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy += 2)
            {
                for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx += 2)
                {
                    int sampleX = centerTileX + dx;

                    int sampleY = centerTileY + dy;

                    if (!mapConfig.IsCoordsValid(sampleX, sampleY))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    TileData tile = gridManager.GetTile(sampleX, sampleY);

                    totalSamples++;

                    if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterShallow || tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                    {
                        waterCount++;
                    }
                    else if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains)
                    {
                        mountainCount++;
                    }
                    else if (tile.Biome == BiomeType.DenseForest || tile.Biome == BiomeType.Taiga)
                    {
                        forestCount++;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        plainsCount++;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (totalSamples == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            float waterFraction = (float)waterCount / totalSamples;

            float forestFraction = (float)forestCount / totalSamples;

            float plainsFraction = (float)plainsCount / totalSamples;

            float mountainFraction = (float)mountainCount / totalSamples;

            float altitude = cameraController.CurrentAltitude;

            float zoomRatio = Mathf.Clamp01(altitude / soundConfig.StrategicAltitudeThreshold);

            bool isWinter = clockManager != null && clockManager.CurrentTime.CurrentSeason == Season.Winter;

            CheckWinterAudioTransition(isWinter);

            float groundFade = 1.0f - (zoomRatio * 0.85f);

            targetWaterVolume = waterFraction * soundConfig.MaxBiomeVolume * groundFade;

            targetForestVolume = forestFraction * soundConfig.MaxBiomeVolume * groundFade;

            targetPlainsVolume = plainsFraction * soundConfig.MaxBiomeVolume * groundFade;

            float altitudeWind = mountainFraction * 0.5f;

            float zoomWind = zoomRatio * 0.75f;

            targetWindVolume = Mathf.Clamp01(altitudeWind + zoomWind) * soundConfig.MaxWindVolume;

            if (isWinter)
            {
                targetForestVolume *= 0.2f;

                targetWaterVolume *= 0.3f;

                targetWindVolume = Mathf.Clamp01(targetWindVolume * 1.35f);
            }

            float master = soundConfig.MasterAmbienceVolume;

            targetWaterVolume *= master;

            targetForestVolume *= master;

            targetPlainsVolume *= master;

            targetWindVolume *= master;
        }


        private void CheckWinterAudioTransition(bool isWinter)
        {
            if (isWinter == wasWinterActive)
            {
                return;
            }

            wasWinterActive = isWinter;

            if (isWinter && soundConfig.WinterWindVariations.Count > 0)
            {
                AssignClipToChannel(windSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.WinterWindVariations));
            }
            else if (!isWinter && soundConfig.WindVariations.Count > 0)
            {
                AssignClipToChannel(windSource, soundConfig.GetRandomClip(soundConfig.WindVariations));
            }
        }


        private void InterpolateAudioVolumes()
        {
            float step = soundConfig.VolumeFadeSpeed * UnityEngine.Time.unscaledDeltaTime;

            plainsSource.volume = Mathf.MoveTowards(plainsSource.volume, targetPlainsVolume, step);

            forestSource.volume = Mathf.MoveTowards(forestSource.volume, targetForestVolume, step);

            waterSource.volume = Mathf.MoveTowards(waterSource.volume, targetWaterVolume, step);

            windSource.volume = Mathf.MoveTowards(windSource.volume, targetWindVolume, step);
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Camera\StrategicCameraController.cs ===
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;

namespace ER.Core.Cameras
{
    [RequireComponent(typeof(Camera))]
    public class StrategicCameraController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static StrategicCameraController Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldMapConfig mapConfig;


        [Header("Zoom Boundaries (Meters)")]
        [SerializeField] private float minZoom = 35.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float maxZoom = 65000.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float zoomSensitivity = 1.25f;

        [SerializeField] private float zoomDamping = 10.0f;


        [Header("Movement Speeds (Relative to Altitude)")]
        [SerializeField] private float basePanSpeed = 1.2f;

        [SerializeField] private float panDamping = 12.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float fastMoveMultiplier = 2.5f;


        private Camera targetCamera;

        private float targetZoomLevel;

        private Vector3 targetPosition;

        private bool isDragging = false;

        private Vector3 dragOriginMousePosition;

        private Vector3 dragOriginWorldPosition;


        public Camera TargetCamera => targetCamera;

        public float CurrentAltitude => targetCamera != null ? (targetCamera.orthographic ? targetCamera.orthographicSize : Mathf.Abs(transform.position.z)) : 100.0f;

        public float TargetZoomLevel => targetZoomLevel;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            targetCamera = GetComponent<Camera>();

            ConfigureCameraClippingPlanes();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            if (mapConfig == null && WorldGridManager.Instance != null)
            {
                mapConfig = WorldGridManager.Instance.MapConfig;
            }

            InitializeCameraPosition();
        }


        private void ConfigureCameraClippingPlanes()
        {
            targetCamera.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;

            targetCamera.farClipPlane = 200000.0f;
        }


        private void InitializeCameraPosition()
        {
            targetPosition = transform.position;

            if (targetCamera.orthographic)
            {
                targetZoomLevel = Mathf.Clamp(targetCamera.orthographicSize, minZoom, maxZoom);

                targetCamera.orthographicSize = targetZoomLevel;
            }
            else
            {
                targetZoomLevel = Mathf.Clamp(Mathf.Abs(transform.position.z), minZoom, maxZoom);

                targetPosition.z = -targetZoomLevel;

                transform.position = targetPosition;
            }
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            HandleZoomInput();

            HandlePanInput();

            HandleMouseDragInput();

            ApplySmoothTransformation();
        }


        private void HandleZoomInput()
        {
            float scroll = Input.GetAxisRaw("Mouse ScrollWheel");

            if (Mathf.Abs(scroll) > 0.001f)
            {
                float zoomFactor = 1.0f - scroll * zoomSensitivity;

                targetZoomLevel = Mathf.Clamp(targetZoomLevel * zoomFactor, minZoom, maxZoom);
            }
        }


        private void HandlePanInput()
        {
            float moveX = Input.GetAxisRaw("Horizontal");

            float moveY = Input.GetAxisRaw("Vertical");

            if (Mathf.Abs(moveX) < 0.001f && Mathf.Abs(moveY) < 0.001f)
            {
                return;
            }

            float speedMultiplier = Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) ? fastMoveMultiplier : 1.0f;

            float currentAlt = CurrentAltitude;

            float dynamicSpeed = currentAlt * basePanSpeed * speedMultiplier;

            Vector3 moveDirection = new Vector3(moveX, moveY, 0.0f).normalized;

            targetPosition += moveDirection * (dynamicSpeed * Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
        }


        private void HandleMouseDragInput()
        {
            if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(2))
            {
                isDragging = true;

                dragOriginMousePosition = Input.mousePosition;

                dragOriginWorldPosition = targetPosition;
            }
            else if (Input.GetMouseButtonUp(2))
            {
                isDragging = false;
            }

            if (isDragging && Input.GetMouseButton(2))
            {
                float currentAlt = CurrentAltitude;

                Vector3 mouseDelta = Input.mousePosition - dragOriginMousePosition;

                float factor = (currentAlt * 2.0f) / Screen.height;

                targetPosition = dragOriginWorldPosition - new Vector3(mouseDelta.x * factor, mouseDelta.y * factor, 0.0f);
            }
        }


        private void ApplySmoothTransformation()
        {
            ClampTargetPositionWithinBounds();

            float deltaTime = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;

            if (targetCamera.orthographic)
            {
                targetCamera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(targetCamera.orthographicSize, targetZoomLevel, deltaTime * zoomDamping);

                Vector3 currentPos = transform.position;

                Vector3 newPos = Vector3.Lerp(currentPos, new Vector3(targetPosition.x, targetPosition.y, currentPos.z), deltaTime * panDamping);

                transform.position = newPos;
            }
            else
            {
                targetPosition.z = -targetZoomLevel;

                transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(transform.position, targetPosition, deltaTime * panDamping);
            }
        }


        private void ClampTargetPositionWithinBounds()
        {
            if (mapConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            float mapLimitX = mapConfig.MapWidth * mapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            float mapLimitY = mapConfig.MapHeight * mapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            targetPosition.x = Mathf.Clamp(targetPosition.x, 0.0f, mapLimitX);

            targetPosition.y = Mathf.Clamp(targetPosition.y, 0.0f, mapLimitY);
        }


        public void FocusOnCoordinates(float worldX, float worldY, float desiredZoom = 120.0f)
        {
            targetPosition.x = worldX;

            targetPosition.y = worldY;

            targetZoomLevel = Mathf.Clamp(desiredZoom, minZoom, maxZoom);
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Chunks\ChunkCullingSystem.cs ===
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.Simulation.Time;

namespace ER.World.Chunks
{
    public class ChunkCullingSystem : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private Camera targetCamera;

        [SerializeField] private ChunkManager chunkManager;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationHotkeysDebugger hotkeysDebugger;


        [Header("Diagnostics")]
        [SerializeField] private int visibleChunksCount = 0;

        [SerializeField] private int totalCulledChunksCount = 0;


        private Bounds cameraFrustumBounds;


        public int VisibleChunksCount => visibleChunksCount;

        public int TotalCulledChunksCount => totalCulledChunksCount;


        private void Start()
        {
            if (targetCamera == null)
            {
                targetCamera = Camera.main;
            }

            if (chunkManager == null)
            {
                chunkManager = ChunkManager.Instance;
            }

            if (hotkeysDebugger == null)
            {
                hotkeysDebugger = FindFirstObjectByType<SimulationHotkeysDebugger>();
            }
        }


        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (targetCamera == null || chunkManager == null || !chunkManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            UpdateCameraBounds();

            ExecuteFrustumCull();
        }


        private void UpdateCameraBounds()
        {
            float verticalSize = targetCamera.orthographicSize * 2.0f;

            float horizontalSize = verticalSize * targetCamera.aspect;

            Vector3 cameraPosition = targetCamera.transform.position;

            Vector3 center = new Vector3(cameraPosition.x, cameraPosition.y, 0.0f);

            Vector3 size = new Vector3(horizontalSize + 32.0f, verticalSize + 32.0f, 50.0f);

            cameraFrustumBounds = new Bounds(center, size);
        }


        private void ExecuteFrustumCull()
        {
            WorldChunk[,] chunks = chunkManager.Chunks;

            int countX = chunks.GetLength(0);

            int countY = chunks.GetLength(1);

            int visibleCounter = 0;

            int culledCounter = 0;

            for (int y = 0; y < countY; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < countX; x++)
                {
                    WorldChunk chunk = chunks[x, y];

                    if (chunk == null)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    bool shouldBeVisible = cameraFrustumBounds.Intersects(chunk.ChunkBounds);

                    chunk.SetVisible(shouldBeVisible);

                    if (shouldBeVisible)
                    {
                        visibleCounter++;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        culledCounter++;
                    }
                }
            }

            visibleChunksCount = visibleCounter;

            totalCulledChunksCount = culledCounter;
        }


        private void OnGUI()
        {
            if (hotkeysDebugger == null || !hotkeysDebugger.ShowDebugOverlay)
            {
                return;
            }

            GUI.Box(new Rect(12, 185, 260, 65), "Viewport Frustum Culling");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 205, 240, 20), $"Visible Chunks: {visibleChunksCount} / 256");

            GUI.Label(new Rect(24, 225, 240, 20), $"Culled (Sleeping): {totalCulledChunksCount} chunks");
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Chunks\ChunkManager.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Visual;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Chunks
{
    public class ChunkManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static ChunkManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration References")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private TilePaletteConfig paletteConfig;

        [SerializeField] private Material chunkMaterial;


        private WorldChunk[,] chunks;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public WorldChunk[,] Chunks => chunks;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null)
            {
                gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;
            }

            if (gridManager != null && !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                gridManager.InitializeGrid();
            }

            if (gridManager != null && gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                InitializeChunkGrid();
            }
        }


        public void InitializeChunkGrid()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int countX = config.ChunksCountX;

            int countY = config.ChunksCountY;

            int chunkSize = config.ChunkSize;

            float tileSize = config.TileWorldSize;

            chunks = new WorldChunk[countX, countY];

            if (chunkMaterial == null)
            {
                Shader unlitColorShader = Shader.Find("Sprites/Default");

                if (unlitColorShader == null) unlitColorShader = Shader.Find("UI/Default");

                chunkMaterial = new Material(unlitColorShader);
            }

            for (int cy = 0; cy < countY; cy++)
            {
                for (int cx = 0; cx < countX; cx++)
                {
                    GameObject chunkObj = new GameObject($"Chunk_{cx}_{cy}");

                    chunkObj.transform.SetParent(transform);

                    float posX = cx * chunkSize * tileSize;

                    float posY = cy * chunkSize * tileSize;

                    chunkObj.transform.position = new Vector3(posX, posY, 0.0f);

                    WorldChunk chunk = chunkObj.AddComponent<WorldChunk>();

                    chunk.Initialize(cx * chunkSize, cy * chunkSize, chunkSize, tileSize, chunkMaterial);

                    chunk.RefreshChunkColors(gridManager.RawTiles, config, paletteConfig, WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes);

                    chunks[cx, cy] = chunk;
                }
            }

            isInitialized = true;

            Debug.Log($"[ChunkManager] Successfully created {countX * countY} chunks spanning {countX * chunkSize * tileSize:F0}x{countY * chunkSize * tileSize:F0} world meters.");
        }


        public bool TryGetChunkAtWorldPos(float worldX, float worldY, out WorldChunk chunk)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || gridManager == null)
            {
                chunk = null;

                return false;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            float chunkWorldSpan = config.ChunkSize * config.TileWorldSize;

            int cx = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldX / chunkWorldSpan);

            int cy = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldY / chunkWorldSpan);

            if (cx >= 0 && cx < config.ChunksCountX && cy >= 0 && cy < config.ChunksCountY)
            {
                chunk = chunks[cx, cy];

                return true;
            }

            chunk = null;

            return false;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Chunks\TilePaletteConfig.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Visual;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Chunks
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "TilePaletteConfig", menuName = "ER/03_World/Visual/Palette Config")]
    public class TilePaletteConfig : ScriptableObject
    {
        [Header("Terrain Base Colors")]
        [SerializeField] private Color32 plainsColor = new Color32(95, 145, 65, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 hillsColor = new Color32(140, 130, 80, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 mountainsColor = new Color32(130, 135, 140, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 waterShallowColor = new Color32(65, 135, 175, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 waterDeepColor = new Color32(35, 80, 135, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 swampColor = new Color32(75, 95, 65, 255);


        [Header("Biome Modifiers")]
        [SerializeField] private Color32 forestTint = new Color32(40, 85, 40, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 taigaTint = new Color32(50, 75, 65, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 steppeTint = new Color32(165, 150, 85, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 desertTint = new Color32(210, 180, 110, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 tundraTint = new Color32(130, 140, 125, 255);


        [Header("Political Overlay Defaults")]
        [SerializeField] private Color32 neutralWildernessColor = new Color32(55, 60, 65, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 contestedTerritoryColor = new Color32(145, 60, 60, 255);


        [Header("Feature Overlays")]
        [SerializeField] private Color32 iceOverlayColor = new Color32(190, 220, 240, 255);

        [SerializeField] private Color32 roadOverlayColor = new Color32(110, 95, 75, 255);


        public Color32 EvaluateTileColor(
            TileData tile,
            WorldMapDisplayMode displayMode = WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<ushort, PolityData> polities = null)
        {
            if (displayMode == WorldMapDisplayMode.PoliticalDeFacto || displayMode == WorldMapDisplayMode.PoliticalDeJure)
            {
                if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains || tile.GeoRegionId == 0)
                {
                    return GetMountainRidgeShade(tile.Elevation);
                }

                if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                {
                    return waterDeepColor;
                }

                ushort polityId = displayMode == WorldMapDisplayMode.PoliticalDeFacto ? tile.DeFactoPolityId : tile.DeJurePolityId;

                if (polityId == 0 || polities == null || !polities.TryGetValue(polityId, out PolityData polity))
                {
                    byte shade = (byte)(tile.Elevation / 10);

                    return new Color32((byte)(neutralWildernessColor.r + shade), (byte)(neutralWildernessColor.g + shade), (byte)(neutralWildernessColor.b + shade), 255);
                }

                Color32 polColor = polity.MapColor;

                byte elevShade = (byte)(tile.Elevation / 8);

                polColor.r = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(polColor.r + elevShade, 0, 255);

                polColor.g = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(polColor.g + elevShade, 0, 255);

                polColor.b = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(polColor.b + elevShade, 0, 255);

                return polColor;
            }

            if (displayMode == WorldMapDisplayMode.GeoRegions)
            {
                if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains || tile.GeoRegionId == 0)
                {
                    return GetMountainRidgeShade(tile.Elevation);
                }

                return GenerateRegionColor(tile.GeoRegionId, tile.Elevation);
            }

            if (tile.HasFlag(TileData.FlagIsFrozen))
            {
                return iceOverlayColor;
            }

            if (tile.HasFlag(TileData.FlagHasRoad))
            {
                return roadOverlayColor;
            }

            Color32 baseColor = GetTerrainBaseColor(tile.Terrain);

            if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Plains || tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Hills)
            {
                baseColor = BlendBiomeColor(baseColor, tile.Biome);
            }

            byte elevationShade = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(tile.Elevation / 8, 0, 30);

            baseColor.r = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(baseColor.r + elevationShade, 0, 255);

            baseColor.g = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(baseColor.g + elevationShade, 0, 255);

            baseColor.b = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(baseColor.b + elevationShade, 0, 255);

            return baseColor;
        }


        private Color32 GetMountainRidgeShade(byte elevation)
        {
            byte shade = (byte)(elevation / 7);

            byte r = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(38 + shade, 0, 255);

            byte g = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(42 + shade, 0, 255);

            byte b = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(48 + shade, 0, 255);

            return new Color32(r, g, b, 255);
        }


        private Color32 GenerateRegionColor(ushort regionId, byte elevation)
        {
            int hash = (regionId * 1664525) + 1013904223;

            if (regionId > 180)
            {
                byte oceanBlue = (byte)(110 + ((hash >> 8) & 0x3F));

                byte oceanGreen = (byte)(60 + ((hash >> 16) & 0x2F));

                return new Color32(20, oceanGreen, oceanBlue, 255);
            }

            byte r = (byte)(80 + (hash & 0x6F));

            byte g = (byte)(80 + ((hash >> 8) & 0x6F));

            byte b = (byte)(70 + ((hash >> 16) & 0x5F));

            byte shade = (byte)(elevation / 9);

            r = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(r + shade, 0, 255);

            g = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(g + shade, 0, 255);

            b = (byte)Mathf.Clamp(b + shade, 0, 255);

            return new Color32(r, g, b, 255);
        }


        private Color32 GetTerrainBaseColor(TerrainType terrain)
        {
            switch (terrain)
            {
                case TerrainType.Plains:
                    return plainsColor;

                case TerrainType.Hills:
                    return hillsColor;

                case TerrainType.Mountains:
                    return mountainsColor;

                case TerrainType.WaterShallow:
                    return waterShallowColor;

                case TerrainType.WaterDeep:
                    return waterDeepColor;

                case TerrainType.Swamp:
                    return swampColor;

                default:
                    return plainsColor;
            }
        }


        private Color32 BlendBiomeColor(Color32 baseColor, BiomeType biome)
        {
            switch (biome)
            {
                case BiomeType.DenseForest:
                    return Color32.Lerp(baseColor, forestTint, 0.45f);

                case BiomeType.Taiga:
                    return Color32.Lerp(baseColor, taigaTint, 0.45f);

                case BiomeType.Steppe:
                    return Color32.Lerp(baseColor, steppeTint, 0.40f);

                case BiomeType.Desert:
                    return Color32.Lerp(baseColor, desertTint, 0.60f);

                case BiomeType.Tundra:
                    return Color32.Lerp(baseColor, tundraTint, 0.50f);

                default:
                    return baseColor;
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Chunks\WorldChunk.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Visual;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Chunks
{
    [RequireComponent(typeof(MeshFilter), typeof(MeshRenderer))]
    public class WorldChunk : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private MeshFilter meshFilter;

        private MeshRenderer meshRenderer;

        private Mesh chunkMesh;

        private Color32[] vertexColors;

        private Bounds chunkBounds;

        private int originX;

        private int originY;

        private int size;

        private float tileSize;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public int OriginX => originX;

        public int OriginY => originY;

        public int Size => size;

        public Bounds ChunkBounds => chunkBounds;


        public void Initialize(int startTileX, int startTileY, int chunkSize, float worldTileSize, Material material)
        {
            originX = startTileX;

            originY = startTileY;

            size = chunkSize;

            tileSize = worldTileSize;

            meshFilter = GetComponent<MeshFilter>();

            meshRenderer = GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();

            meshRenderer.sharedMaterial = material;

            float span = size * tileSize;

            Vector3 center = transform.position + new Vector3(span * 0.5f, span * 0.5f, 0.0f);

            Vector3 boundsSize = new Vector3(span, span, 500.0f);

            chunkBounds = new Bounds(center, boundsSize);

            BuildChunkMesh();

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        public void SetVisible(bool isVisible)
        {
            if (meshRenderer != null && meshRenderer.enabled != isVisible)
            {
                meshRenderer.enabled = isVisible;
            }
        }


        private void BuildChunkMesh()
        {
            chunkMesh = new Mesh
            {
                name = $"ChunkMesh_{originX}_{originY}"
            };

            int totalTiles = size * size;

            int vertexCount = totalTiles * 4;

            int indexCount = totalTiles * 6;

            Vector3[] vertices = new Vector3[vertexCount];

            Vector2[] uvs = new Vector2[vertexCount];

            int[] triangles = new int[indexCount];

            vertexColors = new Color32[vertexCount];

            int vIndex = 0;

            int tIndex = 0;

            for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    float x0 = x * tileSize;

                    float y0 = y * tileSize;

                    float x1 = (x + 1) * tileSize;

                    float y1 = (y + 1) * tileSize;

                    vertices[vIndex + 0] = new Vector3(x0, y0, 0.0f);

                    vertices[vIndex + 1] = new Vector3(x1, y0, 0.0f);

                    vertices[vIndex + 2] = new Vector3(x0, y1, 0.0f);

                    vertices[vIndex + 3] = new Vector3(x1, y1, 0.0f);

                    uvs[vIndex + 0] = new Vector2(0.0f, 0.0f);

                    uvs[vIndex + 1] = new Vector2(1.0f, 0.0f);

                    uvs[vIndex + 2] = new Vector2(0.0f, 1.0f);

                    uvs[vIndex + 3] = new Vector2(1.0f, 1.0f);

                    triangles[tIndex + 0] = vIndex + 0;

                    triangles[tIndex + 1] = vIndex + 2;

                    triangles[tIndex + 2] = vIndex + 1;

                    triangles[tIndex + 3] = vIndex + 1;

                    triangles[tIndex + 4] = vIndex + 2;

                    triangles[tIndex + 5] = vIndex + 3;

                    vIndex += 4;

                    tIndex += 6;
                }
            }

            chunkMesh.vertices = vertices;

            chunkMesh.uv = uvs;

            chunkMesh.triangles = triangles;

            chunkMesh.colors32 = vertexColors;

            chunkMesh.RecalculateBounds();

            meshFilter.sharedMesh = chunkMesh;
        }


        public void RefreshChunkColors(
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig config,
            TilePaletteConfig palette,
            WorldMapDisplayMode displayMode,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<ushort, PolityData> polities = null)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || !tiles.IsCreated || chunkMesh == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            int vIndex = 0;

            for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
            {
                int globalY = originY + y;

                for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    int globalX = originX + x;

                    Color32 tileColor = new Color32(40, 40, 40, 255);

                    if (config.IsCoordsValid(globalX, globalY))
                    {
                        int globalIndex = config.CoordsToIndex(globalX, globalY);

                        TileData tile = tiles[globalIndex];

                        tileColor = ResolveTileColor(tile, palette, displayMode, polities);
                    }

                    vertexColors[vIndex + 0] = tileColor;

                    vertexColors[vIndex + 1] = tileColor;

                    vertexColors[vIndex + 2] = tileColor;

                    vertexColors[vIndex + 3] = tileColor;

                    vIndex += 4;
                }
            }

            chunkMesh.colors32 = vertexColors;
        }


        private Color32 ResolveTileColor(
            TileData tile,
            TilePaletteConfig palette,
            WorldMapDisplayMode mode,
            IReadOnlyDictionary<ushort, PolityData> polities)
        {
            if (palette == null)
            {
                return new Color32(80, 80, 80, 255);
            }

            return palette.EvaluateTileColor(tile, mode, polities);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Fauna
{
    public enum AnimalType : byte
    {
        Reindeer = 0,
        WildBoar = 1
    }

    public enum AnimalState : byte
    {
        Grazing = 0,
        Fleeing = 1,
        Exhausted = 2,
        Carcass = 3
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct AnimalData
    {
        public int AnimalId;

        public ushort GeoRegionId;

        public AnimalType Type;

        public AnimalState State;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public float TargetPositionX;

        public float TargetPositionY;

        public float PastureCenterX;

        public float PastureCenterY;

        public float VelocityX;

        public float VelocityY;

        public float Stamina;

        public byte Health;

        public byte MaxHealth;

        public uint RemainingMeat;

        public uint RemainingHides;

        public bool IsActive;


        public static AnimalData Create(
            int id,
            ushort regionId,
            AnimalType type,
            float x,
            float y)
        {
            byte hp = type == AnimalType.Reindeer ? (byte)75 : (byte)120;

            uint meat = type == AnimalType.Reindeer ? 35u : 55u;

            uint hides = type == AnimalType.Reindeer ? 6u : 4u;

            return new AnimalData
            {
                AnimalId = id,
                GeoRegionId = regionId,
                Type = type,
                State = AnimalState.Grazing,
                WorldPositionX = x,
                WorldPositionY = y,
                TargetPositionX = x,
                TargetPositionY = y,
                PastureCenterX = x,
                PastureCenterY = y,
                VelocityX = 0.0f,
                VelocityY = 0.0f,
                Stamina = 100.0f,
                Health = hp,
                MaxHealth = hp,
                RemainingMeat = meat,
                RemainingHides = hides,
                IsActive = true
            };
        }
    }
}
