using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Mathematics;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.Simulation.Time;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Polity;
using ER.World.Population;

namespace ER.World.Fauna
{
    public class AnimalHerdManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static AnimalHerdManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Herd Balance Parameters")]
        [SerializeField] private int herdCountPerSettlement = 3;

        [SerializeField] private int animalsPerHerd = 6;

        [SerializeField] private float minSpawnDistance = 35.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float maxSpawnDistance = 85.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float alertRadius = 14.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float walkSpeed = 0.65f;

        [SerializeField] private float fleeSpeed = 2.10f;

        [SerializeField] private float pastureTerritoryRadius = 55.0f;


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;

        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private PopulationManager populationManager;


        [Header("Debug Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool drawFaunaGizmos = true;


        private NativeList<AnimalData> animals;

        private int nextAnimalId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public NativeList<AnimalData> RawAnimals => animals;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeStorage();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (clockManager == null) clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (populationManager == null) populationManager = PopulationManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager != null)
            {
                polityManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (polityManager == null || !polityManager.IsInitialized || polityManager.Anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            SpawnInitialHerds();

            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnTick += HandleFaunaTick;
            }

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void InitializeStorage()
        {
            if (!animals.IsCreated)
            {
                animals = new NativeList<AnimalData>(512, Allocator.Persistent);
            }
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnTick -= HandleFaunaTick;
            }

            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void DisposeStorage()
        {
            if (animals.IsCreated)
            {
                animals.Dispose();
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        private void SpawnInitialHerds()
        {
            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            for (int a = 0; a < anchors.Count; a++)
            {
                PolityAnchorData anchor = anchors[a];

                float centerX = (anchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float centerY = (anchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                for (int h = 0; h < herdCountPerSettlement; h++)
                {
                    Vector2 herdOffset = UnityEngine.Random.insideUnitCircle.normalized * UnityEngine.Random.Range(minSpawnDistance, maxSpawnDistance);

                    float herdCenterX = centerX + herdOffset.x;

                    float herdCenterY = centerY + herdOffset.y;

                    AnimalType herdType = (h % 2 == 0) ? AnimalType.Reindeer : AnimalType.WildBoar;

                    for (int i = 0; i < animalsPerHerd; i++)
                    {
                        Vector2 scatter = UnityEngine.Random.insideUnitCircle * 7.0f;

                        int id = nextAnimalId++;

                        AnimalData animal = AnimalData.Create(id, anchor.GeoRegionId, herdType, herdCenterX + scatter.x, herdCenterY + scatter.y);

                        animals.Add(animal);
                    }
                }
            }

            Debug.Log($"[AnimalHerdManager] Successfully spawned {animals.Length} wild game animals tethered to valley pastures.");
        }


        private void HandleFaunaTick(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || animals.Length == 0 || populationManager == null || gridManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            NativeArray<PopData> pops = populationManager.ActivePops;

            int popCount = populationManager.TotalPopulationCount;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            float minBoundX = 35.0f;

            float maxBoundX = (gridManager.MapConfig.MapWidth * tileSize) - 35.0f;

            float minBoundY = 35.0f;

            float maxBoundY = (gridManager.MapConfig.MapHeight * tileSize) - 35.0f;

            for (int i = 0; i < animals.Length; i++)
            {
                AnimalData animal = animals[i];

                if (!animal.IsActive || animal.State == AnimalState.Carcass)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                float2 animalPos = new float2(animal.WorldPositionX, animal.WorldPositionY);

                float2 pastureCenter = new float2(animal.PastureCenterX, animal.PastureCenterY);

                float minPopDist = float.MaxValue;

                float2 nearestPopPos = float2.zero;

                for (int p = 0; p < popCount; p++)
                {
                    PopData pop = pops[p];

                    if (!pop.IsAlive) continue;

                    float dist = math.distance(animalPos, new float2(pop.WorldPositionX, pop.WorldPositionY));

                    if (dist < minPopDist)
                    {
                        minPopDist = dist;

                        nearestPopPos = new float2(pop.WorldPositionX, pop.WorldPositionY);
                    }
                }

                if (animal.State == AnimalState.Exhausted)
                {
                    animal.Stamina = math.min(100.0f, animal.Stamina + 4.0f);

                    if (animal.Stamina >= 60.0f)
                    {
                        animal.State = AnimalState.Grazing;
                    }
                }
                else if (minPopDist < alertRadius)
                {
                    animal.State = AnimalState.Fleeing;

                    animal.Stamina = math.max(0.0f, animal.Stamina - 5.5f);

                    if (animal.Stamina <= 0.0f)
                    {
                        animal.State = AnimalState.Exhausted;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        float2 fleeDir = math.normalize(animalPos - nearestPopPos);

                        if (math.distance(animalPos, pastureCenter) > pastureTerritoryRadius)
                        {
                            float2 backToPastureDir = math.normalize(pastureCenter - animalPos);

                            fleeDir = math.normalize(fleeDir + backToPastureDir * 1.5f);
                        }

                        animal.TargetPositionX = Mathf.Clamp(animal.WorldPositionX + fleeDir.x * 16.0f, minBoundX, maxBoundX);

                        animal.TargetPositionY = Mathf.Clamp(animal.WorldPositionY + fleeDir.y * 16.0f, minBoundY, maxBoundY);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    animal.Stamina = math.min(100.0f, animal.Stamina + 2.0f);

                    if (animal.State == AnimalState.Fleeing && minPopDist > alertRadius * 1.5f)
                    {
                        animal.State = AnimalState.Grazing;
                    }
                }

                float currentSpeed = walkSpeed;

                if (animal.State == AnimalState.Fleeing)
                {
                    currentSpeed = fleeSpeed;
                }
                else if (animal.State == AnimalState.Exhausted)
                {
                    currentSpeed = walkSpeed * 0.45f;
                }

                float2 targetPos = new float2(animal.TargetPositionX, animal.TargetPositionY);

                float distToTarget = math.distance(animalPos, targetPos);

                if (distToTarget > 0.5f)
                {
                    float2 moveDir = math.normalize(targetPos - animalPos);

                    animal.WorldPositionX = Mathf.Clamp(animal.WorldPositionX + moveDir.x * currentSpeed, minBoundX, maxBoundX);

                    animal.WorldPositionY = Mathf.Clamp(animal.WorldPositionY + moveDir.y * currentSpeed, minBoundY, maxBoundY);

                    animal.VelocityX = moveDir.x * currentSpeed;

                    animal.VelocityY = moveDir.y * currentSpeed;
                }
                else if (animal.State == AnimalState.Grazing && UnityEngine.Random.value < 0.06f)
                {
                    Vector2 roam = UnityEngine.Random.insideUnitCircle * 8.0f;

                    animal.TargetPositionX = Mathf.Clamp(animal.PastureCenterX + roam.x, minBoundX, maxBoundX);

                    animal.TargetPositionY = Mathf.Clamp(animal.PastureCenterY + roam.y, minBoundY, maxBoundY);
                }

                animals[i] = animal;
            }
        }


        public bool TryGetAnimal(int animalId, out AnimalData animal, out int arrayIndex)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < animals.Length; i++)
            {
                if (animals[i].AnimalId == animalId && animals[i].IsActive)
                {
                    animal = animals[i];

                    arrayIndex = i;

                    return true;
                }
            }

            animal = default;

            arrayIndex = -1;

            return false;
        }


        public void SetAnimal(int index, AnimalData animal)
        {
            if (index >= 0 && index < animals.Length)
            {
                animals[index] = animal;
            }
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!drawFaunaGizmos || !animals.IsCreated || animals.Length == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < animals.Length; i++)
            {
                AnimalData a = animals[i];

                if (!a.IsActive) continue;

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(a.WorldPositionX, a.WorldPositionY, -2.4f);

                if (a.State == AnimalState.Carcass)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.6f, 0.1f, 0.1f, 0.85f);

                    Gizmos.DrawCube(pos, new Vector3(1.3f, 0.7f, 0.4f));

                    continue;
                }

                if (a.State == AnimalState.Exhausted)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = Color.yellow;
                }
                else
                {
                    Gizmos.color = a.Type == AnimalType.Reindeer ? new Color(0.75f, 0.55f, 0.35f) : new Color(0.35f, 0.25f, 0.18f);
                }

                Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, 0.95f);

                if (a.State == AnimalState.Fleeing)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = Color.red;

                    Gizmos.DrawLine(pos, pos + new Vector3(a.VelocityX, a.VelocityY, 0.0f) * 2.0f);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Grid\TileData.cs ===
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Grid
{
    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct TileData
    {
        public TerrainType Terrain;
        public BiomeType Biome;
        public byte MovementCost;
        public byte Elevation;

        public byte Moisture;
        public byte Fertility;
        public byte PopCount;
        public byte Flags;

        public ushort GeoRegionId;
        public ushort DeFactoPolityId;
        public ushort DeJurePolityId;
        public ushort ResourceReserve;

        public const byte FlagNone = 0;
        public const byte FlagHasRoad = 1 << 0;
        public const byte FlagHasRiver = 1 << 1;
        public const byte FlagIsFrozen = 1 << 2;
        public const byte FlagHasBuilding = 1 << 3;
        public const byte FlagIsOnFire = 1 << 4;

        public static TileData CreateDefault()
        {
            return new TileData
            {
                Terrain = TerrainType.Plains,
                Biome = BiomeType.TemperatePlains,
                MovementCost = 10,
                Elevation = 0,

                Moisture = 128,
                Fertility = 128,
                PopCount = 0,
                Flags = FlagNone,

                GeoRegionId = 0,
                DeFactoPolityId = 0,
                DeJurePolityId = 0,
                ResourceReserve = 1000
            };
        }

        public bool HasFlag(byte flag)
        {
            return (Flags & flag) != 0;
        }

        public void SetFlag(byte flag, bool value)
        {
            if (value)
            {
                Flags |= flag;
            }
            else
            {
                Flags &= (byte)~flag;
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Grid\WorldGridManager.cs ===
using System;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Grid
{
    public class WorldGridManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static WorldGridManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration Asset")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldMapConfig mapConfig;


        [Header("Procedural Generation Parameters")]
        [SerializeField] private int seed = 1337;

        [SerializeField] private float elevationFrequency = 0.004f;

        [SerializeField] private float ridgeFrequencyMultiplier = 2.4f;

        [SerializeField] private float moistureFrequency = 0.003f;

        [SerializeField] private float mountainThreshold = 0.68f;

        [SerializeField] private float hillsThreshold = 0.54f;

        [SerializeField] private float waterShallowThreshold = 0.32f;

        [SerializeField] private float waterDeepThreshold = 0.22f;


        private NativeArray<TileData> tiles;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public WorldMapConfig MapConfig => mapConfig;

        public NativeArray<TileData> RawTiles => tiles;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeGrid();
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            DisposeGrid();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeGrid();
        }


        public void InitializeGrid()
        {
            if (isInitialized || mapConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            int totalTiles = mapConfig.TotalTilesCount;

            tiles = new NativeArray<TileData>(totalTiles, Allocator.Persistent);

            GenerateInitialTerrain();

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void DisposeGrid()
        {
            if (!tiles.IsCreated)
            {
                return;
            }

            tiles.Dispose();

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        private void GenerateInitialTerrain()
        {
            int width = mapConfig.MapWidth;

            int height = mapConfig.MapHeight;

            float seedOffsetX = (seed ^ 0x5DEECE66D) % 10000;

            float seedOffsetY = (seed ^ 0xB) % 10000;

            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
                {
                    int index = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

                    float baseSampleX = (x + seedOffsetX) * elevationFrequency;

                    float baseSampleY = (y + seedOffsetY) * elevationFrequency;

                    float continentBase = Mathf.PerlinNoise(baseSampleX, baseSampleY);

                    float ridgeSampleX = (x + seedOffsetX + 2345.6f) * (elevationFrequency * ridgeFrequencyMultiplier);

                    float ridgeSampleY = (y + seedOffsetY + 6789.1f) * (elevationFrequency * ridgeFrequencyMultiplier);

                    float rawRidgeNoise = Mathf.PerlinNoise(ridgeSampleX, ridgeSampleY);

                    float sharpRidge = 1.0f - Mathf.Abs(rawRidgeNoise * 2.0f - 1.0f);

                    sharpRidge = sharpRidge * sharpRidge;

                    float elevationValue;

                    if (continentBase < waterShallowThreshold)
                    {
                        elevationValue = continentBase;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        float landFactor = Mathf.Clamp01((continentBase - waterShallowThreshold) / (1.0f - waterShallowThreshold));

                        elevationValue = Mathf.Clamp01(continentBase + sharpRidge * 0.42f * landFactor);
                    }

                    float moistureSampleX = (x + seedOffsetX + 5000) * moistureFrequency;

                    float moistureSampleY = (y + seedOffsetY + 5000) * moistureFrequency;

                    float moistureValue = Mathf.PerlinNoise(moistureSampleX, moistureSampleY);

                    TileData tile = TileData.CreateDefault();

                    tile.Elevation = (byte)(elevationValue * 255.0f);

                    tile.Moisture = (byte)(moistureValue * 255.0f);

                    if (elevationValue < waterDeepThreshold)
                    {
                        tile.Terrain = TerrainType.WaterDeep;
                        tile.MovementCost = 255;
                        tile.Fertility = 0;
                        tile.ResourceReserve = 0;
                    }
                    else if (elevationValue < waterShallowThreshold)
                    {
                        tile.Terrain = TerrainType.WaterShallow;
                        tile.MovementCost = 120;
                        tile.Fertility = 30;
                        tile.ResourceReserve = 500;
                        tile.SetFlag(TileData.FlagHasRiver, true);
                    }
                    else if (elevationValue > mountainThreshold)
                    {
                        tile.Terrain = TerrainType.Mountains;
                        tile.MovementCost = 240;
                        tile.Fertility = 5;
                        tile.ResourceReserve = 5000;
                    }
                    else if (elevationValue > hillsThreshold)
                    {
                        tile.Terrain = TerrainType.Hills;
                        tile.MovementCost = 45;
                        tile.Fertility = 60;
                        tile.ResourceReserve = 2500;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (moistureValue > 0.65f && elevationValue < 0.40f)
                        {
                            tile.Terrain = TerrainType.Swamp;
                            tile.MovementCost = 70;
                            tile.Fertility = 90;
                            tile.ResourceReserve = 1200;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            tile.Terrain = TerrainType.Plains;
                            tile.MovementCost = 10;
                            tile.Fertility = 160;
                            tile.ResourceReserve = 1500;
                        }
                    }

                    tile.Biome = DetermineBiome(tile.Terrain, tile.Elevation, tile.Moisture);

                    tiles[index] = tile;
                }
            }
        }


        private BiomeType DetermineBiome(TerrainType terrain, byte elevation, byte moisture)
        {
            if (terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep || terrain == TerrainType.WaterShallow)
            {
                return BiomeType.TemperatePlains;
            }

            if (elevation > 200)
            {
                return BiomeType.Tundra;
            }

            if (moisture < 60)
            {
                return BiomeType.Steppe;
            }

            if (moisture < 100)
            {
                return BiomeType.Desert;
            }

            if (moisture > 160)
            {
                return elevation > 130 ? BiomeType.Taiga : BiomeType.DenseForest;
            }

            return BiomeType.TemperatePlains;
        }


        public TileData GetTile(int x, int y)
        {
            int index = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

            return tiles[index];
        }


        public TileData GetTile(int index)
        {
            return tiles[index];
        }


        public void SetTile(int x, int y, TileData data)
        {
            int index = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

            tiles[index] = data;
        }


        public void SetTile(int index, TileData data)
        {
            tiles[index] = data;
        }


        public bool TryGetTile(int x, int y, out TileData tile)
        {
            if (!mapConfig.IsCoordsValid(x, y))
            {
                tile = default;
                return false;
            }

            int index = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

            tile = tiles[index];

            return true;
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Grid
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "WorldMapConfig", menuName = "ER/03_World/Config/World Map Config")]
    public class WorldMapConfig : ScriptableObject
    {
        [Header("Global Grid Dimensions")]
        [SerializeField] private int mapWidth = 1000;

        [SerializeField] private int mapHeight = 1000;

        [SerializeField] private float tileWorldSize = 100.0f;


        [Header("Chunk Grid Dimensions")]
        [SerializeField] private int chunkSize = 50;


        public int MapWidth => mapWidth;

        public int MapHeight => mapHeight;

        public float TileWorldSize => tileWorldSize;

        public int TotalTilesCount => mapWidth * mapHeight;

        public int ChunkSize => chunkSize;

        public int ChunksCountX => mapWidth / chunkSize;

        public int ChunksCountY => mapHeight / chunkSize;


        public int CoordsToIndex(int x, int y)
        {
            return y * mapWidth + x;
        }


        public void IndexToCoords(int index, out int x, out int y)
        {
            x = index % mapWidth;

            y = index / mapWidth;
        }


        public bool IsCoordsValid(int x, int y)
        {
            return x >= 0 && x < mapWidth && y >= 0 && y < mapHeight;
        }


        public Vector2 TileToWorldPosition(int x, int y)
        {
            return new Vector2((x + 0.5f) * tileWorldSize, (y + 0.5f) * tileWorldSize);
        }


        public void WorldToTileCoords(float worldX, float worldY, out int tileX, out int tileY)
        {
            tileX = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldX / tileWorldSize);

            tileY = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldY / tileWorldSize);
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Navigation\Data\MacroNavData.cs ===
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Unity.Mathematics;

namespace ER.World.Navigation
{
    public enum MacroNodeType : byte
    {
        RegionCenter = 0,
        MountainPass = 1,
        RiverFord = 2,
        CoastalGate = 3
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct MacroNavNode
    {
        public int NodeId;

        public ushort GeoRegionIdA;

        public ushort GeoRegionIdB;

        public MacroNodeType NodeType;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public float Elevation;

        public bool IsPassable;


        public static MacroNavNode Create(
            int id,
            ushort regionA,
            ushort regionB,
            MacroNodeType type,
            float x,
            float y,
            float elevation)
        {
            return new MacroNavNode
            {
                NodeId = id,
                GeoRegionIdA = regionA,
                GeoRegionIdB = regionB,
                NodeType = type,
                WorldPositionX = x,
                WorldPositionY = y,
                Elevation = elevation,
                IsPassable = true
            };
        }
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct MacroNavEdge
    {
        public int EdgeId;

        public int FromNodeId;

        public int ToNodeId;

        public float PhysicalLengthMeters;

        public float TraversalWeight;

        public bool IsBlocked;


        public static MacroNavEdge Create(
            int edgeId,
            int from,
            int to,
            float length,
            float weight)
        {
            return new MacroNavEdge
            {
                EdgeId = edgeId,
                FromNodeId = from,
                ToNodeId = to,
                PhysicalLengthMeters = length,
                TraversalWeight = weight,
                IsBlocked = false
            };
        }
    }

    public struct MacroPathResult
    {
        public bool Success;

        public float TotalDistanceMeters;

        public float TotalTraversalCost;

        public int[] NodeIds;

        public float2[] Waypoints;
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Navigation\Graph\MacroNavGraph.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Mathematics;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Navigation
{
    public class MacroNavGraph
    {
        private readonly Dictionary<int, MacroNavNode> nodes = new Dictionary<int, MacroNavNode>();

        private readonly Dictionary<int, List<MacroNavEdge>> adjacencyList = new Dictionary<int, List<MacroNavEdge>>();

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, int> regionCenterLookup = new Dictionary<ushort, int>();


        public IReadOnlyDictionary<int, MacroNavNode> Nodes => nodes;

        public int NodeCount => nodes.Count;


        public void Clear()
        {
            nodes.Clear();

            adjacencyList.Clear();

            regionCenterLookup.Clear();
        }


        public void AddNode(MacroNavNode node)
        {
            nodes[node.NodeId] = node;

            if (!adjacencyList.ContainsKey(node.NodeId))
            {
                adjacencyList[node.NodeId] = new List<MacroNavEdge>();
            }

            if (node.NodeType == MacroNodeType.RegionCenter && node.GeoRegionIdA > 0)
            {
                regionCenterLookup[node.GeoRegionIdA] = node.NodeId;
            }
        }


        public void AddEdge(MacroNavEdge edge)
        {
            if (adjacencyList.ContainsKey(edge.FromNodeId))
            {
                adjacencyList[edge.FromNodeId].Add(edge);
            }
        }


        public bool TryGetRegionCenterNodeId(ushort regionId, out int nodeId)
        {
            return regionCenterLookup.TryGetValue(regionId, out nodeId);
        }


        public MacroPathResult FindPath(int startNodeId, int targetNodeId)
        {
            MacroPathResult result = new MacroPathResult
            {
                Success = false,
                TotalDistanceMeters = 0.0f,
                TotalTraversalCost = 0.0f,
                NodeIds = Array.Empty<int>(),
                Waypoints = Array.Empty<float2>()
            };

            if (!nodes.ContainsKey(startNodeId) || !nodes.ContainsKey(targetNodeId))
            {
                return result;
            }

            if (startNodeId == targetNodeId)
            {
                MacroNavNode singleNode = nodes[startNodeId];

                result.Success = true;

                result.NodeIds = new[] { startNodeId };

                result.Waypoints = new[] { new float2(singleNode.WorldPositionX, singleNode.WorldPositionY) };

                return result;
            }

            Dictionary<int, float> gScore = new Dictionary<int, float>();

            Dictionary<int, float> fScore = new Dictionary<int, float>();

            Dictionary<int, int> cameFrom = new Dictionary<int, int>();

            List<int> openSet = new List<int>();

            HashSet<int> closedSet = new HashSet<int>();

            MacroNavNode targetNode = nodes[targetNodeId];

            float2 targetPos = new float2(targetNode.WorldPositionX, targetNode.WorldPositionY);

            gScore[startNodeId] = 0.0f;

            fScore[startNodeId] = Heuristic(nodes[startNodeId], targetPos);

            openSet.Add(startNodeId);

            while (openSet.Count > 0)
            {
                int current = GetLowestFScoreNode(openSet, fScore);

                if (current == targetNodeId)
                {
                    return ReconstructPath(current, cameFrom, gScore[targetNodeId]);
                }

                openSet.Remove(current);

                closedSet.Add(current);

                if (!adjacencyList.TryGetValue(current, out List<MacroNavEdge> edges))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                for (int i = 0; i < edges.Count; i++)
                {
                    MacroNavEdge edge = edges[i];

                    if (edge.IsBlocked) continue;

                    int neighbor = edge.ToNodeId;

                    if (closedSet.Contains(neighbor)) continue;

                    if (!nodes.TryGetValue(neighbor, out MacroNavNode neighborNode) || !neighborNode.IsPassable)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    float tentativeG = gScore[current] + edge.TraversalWeight;

                    if (!gScore.ContainsKey(neighbor) || tentativeG < gScore[neighbor])
                    {
                        cameFrom[neighbor] = current;

                        gScore[neighbor] = tentativeG;

                        fScore[neighbor] = tentativeG + Heuristic(neighborNode, targetPos);

                        if (!openSet.Contains(neighbor))
                        {
                            openSet.Add(neighbor);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            return result;
        }


        private float Heuristic(MacroNavNode node, float2 targetPos)
        {
            float2 nodePos = new float2(node.WorldPositionX, node.WorldPositionY);

            return math.distance(nodePos, targetPos);
        }


        private int GetLowestFScoreNode(List<int> openSet, Dictionary<int, float> fScore)
        {
            int bestNode = openSet[0];

            float lowestF = fScore.ContainsKey(bestNode) ? fScore[bestNode] : float.MaxValue;

            for (int i = 1; i < openSet.Count; i++)
            {
                int candidate = openSet[i];

                float score = fScore.ContainsKey(candidate) ? fScore[candidate] : float.MaxValue;

                if (score < lowestF)
                {
                    lowestF = score;

                    bestNode = candidate;
                }
            }

            return bestNode;
        }


        private MacroPathResult ReconstructPath(int current, Dictionary<int, int> cameFrom, float totalCost)
        {
            List<int> pathNodes = new List<int> { current };

            while (cameFrom.ContainsKey(current))
            {
                current = cameFrom[current];

                pathNodes.Add(current);
            }

            pathNodes.Reverse();

            float2[] waypoints = new float2[pathNodes.Count];

            float totalDist = 0.0f;

            for (int i = 0; i < pathNodes.Count; i++)
            {
                MacroNavNode n = nodes[pathNodes[i]];

                waypoints[i] = new float2(n.WorldPositionX, n.WorldPositionY);

                if (i > 0)
                {
                    totalDist += math.distance(waypoints[i - 1], waypoints[i]);
                }
            }

            return new MacroPathResult
            {
                Success = true,
                TotalDistanceMeters = totalDist,
                TotalTraversalCost = totalCost,
                NodeIds = pathNodes.ToArray(),
                Waypoints = waypoints
            };
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Navigation\Management\MacroPathfindingManager.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Mathematics;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Regions;

namespace ER.World.Navigation
{
    public class MacroPathfindingManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static MacroPathfindingManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private GeoRegionManager regionManager;


        [Header("Pass Detection Calibration")]
        [SerializeField] private int borderSamplingStep = 2;

        [SerializeField] private float elevationSlopePenalty = 1.6f;


        [Header("Debug Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool drawMacroGraphGizmos = true;

        [SerializeField] private bool drawTestedPathGizmo = false;


        private readonly MacroNavGraph graph = new MacroNavGraph();

        private MacroPathResult lastDebugPath;

        private int nextNodeId = 1;

        private int nextEdgeId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public MacroNavGraph Graph => graph;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (regionManager == null) regionManager = GeoRegionManager.Instance;

            if (regionManager != null)
            {
                regionManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized || regionManager == null || !regionManager.IsSegmented)
            {
                return;
            }

            BakeMacroNavGraph();

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        public void BakeMacroNavGraph()
        {
            graph.Clear();

            nextNodeId = 1;

            nextEdgeId = 1;

            var regions = regionManager.Regions;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            Dictionary<ushort, int> centerNodeMap = new Dictionary<ushort, int>();

            for (int i = 0; i < regions.Count; i++)
            {
                GeoRegionData reg = regions[i];

                if (reg.IsOceanRegion || reg.RegionId == 0) continue;

                int tIdx = config.CoordsToIndex(reg.CenterTileX, reg.CenterTileY);

                float elev = tiles[tIdx].Elevation;

                float wx = (reg.CenterTileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float wy = (reg.CenterTileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                int cNodeId = nextNodeId++;

                MacroNavNode centerNode = MacroNavNode.Create(cNodeId, reg.RegionId, 0, MacroNodeType.RegionCenter, wx, wy, elev);

                graph.AddNode(centerNode);

                centerNodeMap[reg.RegionId] = cNodeId;
            }

            Dictionary<long, BorderPassCandidate> borderPasses = DetectMountainPassPortals(regions, config, tiles, tileSize);

            foreach (var kvp in borderPasses)
            {
                BorderPassCandidate cand = kvp.Value;

                int passNodeId = nextNodeId++;

                MacroNavNode passNode = MacroNavNode.Create(
                    passNodeId,
                    cand.RegionA,
                    cand.RegionB,
                    MacroNodeType.MountainPass,
                    cand.WorldPos.x,
                    cand.WorldPos.y,
                    cand.Elevation);

                graph.AddNode(passNode);

                if (centerNodeMap.TryGetValue(cand.RegionA, out int centerA))
                {
                    ConnectBidirectional(centerA, passNodeId);
                }

                if (centerNodeMap.TryGetValue(cand.RegionB, out int centerB))
                {
                    ConnectBidirectional(centerB, passNodeId);
                }
            }

            Debug.Log($"[MacroPathfindingManager] Successfully baked MacroNavGraph with {graph.NodeCount} nodes across {regions.Count} valleys.");
        }


        private Dictionary<long, BorderPassCandidate> DetectMountainPassPortals(
            IReadOnlyList<GeoRegionData> regions,
            WorldMapConfig config,
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            float tileSize)
        {
            Dictionary<long, BorderPassCandidate> passes = new Dictionary<long, BorderPassCandidate>();

            int width = config.MapWidth;

            int height = config.MapHeight;

            for (int y = 5; y < height - 5; y += borderSamplingStep)
            {
                for (int x = 5; x < width - 5; x += borderSamplingStep)
                {
                    int idx = y * width + x;

                    TileData tCurr = tiles[idx];

                    if (tCurr.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep || tCurr.GeoRegionId == 0) continue;

                    CheckBorderCandidate(x + 1, y, tCurr, config, tiles, tileSize, passes);

                    CheckBorderCandidate(x, y + 1, tCurr, config, tiles, tileSize, passes);
                }
            }

            return passes;
        }


        private void CheckBorderCandidate(
            int nx,
            int ny,
            TileData tCurr,
            WorldMapConfig config,
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            float tileSize,
            Dictionary<long, BorderPassCandidate> passes)
        {
            if (!config.IsCoordsValid(nx, ny)) return;

            int nIdx = config.CoordsToIndex(nx, ny);

            TileData tNext = tiles[nIdx];

            if (tNext.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep || tNext.GeoRegionId == 0) return;

            if (tCurr.GeoRegionId == tNext.GeoRegionId) return;

            if (tCurr.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains && tNext.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains)
            {
                if (tCurr.Elevation > 210 && tNext.Elevation > 210) return;
            }

            ushort rA = Math.Min(tCurr.GeoRegionId, tNext.GeoRegionId);

            ushort rB = Math.Max(tCurr.GeoRegionId, tNext.GeoRegionId);

            long pairKey = ((long)rA << 32) | rB;

            float avgElev = (tCurr.Elevation + tNext.Elevation) * 0.5f;

            float wx = (nx + 0.5f) * tileSize;

            float wy = (ny + 0.5f) * tileSize;

            if (!passes.TryGetValue(pairKey, out BorderPassCandidate existing) || avgElev < existing.Elevation)
            {
                passes[pairKey] = new BorderPassCandidate
                {
                    RegionA = rA,
                    RegionB = rB,
                    WorldPos = new Vector2(wx, wy),
                    Elevation = avgElev
                };
            }
        }


        private void ConnectBidirectional(int nodeAId, int nodeBId)
        {
            MacroNavNode a = graph.Nodes[nodeAId];

            MacroNavNode b = graph.Nodes[nodeBId];

            float dist = math.distance(new float2(a.WorldPositionX, a.WorldPositionY), new float2(b.WorldPositionX, b.WorldPositionY));

            float elevDiff = Mathf.Abs(a.Elevation - b.Elevation);

            float weight = dist + (elevDiff * elevationSlopePenalty * 15.0f);

            int e1 = nextEdgeId++;

            int e2 = nextEdgeId++;

            graph.AddEdge(MacroNavEdge.Create(e1, nodeAId, nodeBId, dist, weight));

            graph.AddEdge(MacroNavEdge.Create(e2, nodeBId, nodeAId, dist, weight));
        }


        public MacroPathResult FindMacroPathBetweenRegions(ushort startRegionId, ushort targetRegionId)
        {
            if (!isInitialized)
            {
                return default;
            }

            if (!graph.TryGetRegionCenterNodeId(startRegionId, out int startNode) ||
                !graph.TryGetRegionCenterNodeId(targetRegionId, out int targetNode))
            {
                return default;
            }

            MacroPathResult path = graph.FindPath(startNode, targetNode);

            lastDebugPath = path;

            return path;
        }


        public MacroPathResult FindMacroPathBetweenPositions(Vector2 startPos, Vector2 targetPos)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || gridManager == null)
            {
                return default;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int sx = Mathf.FloorToInt(startPos.x / config.TileWorldSize);

            int sy = Mathf.FloorToInt(startPos.y / config.TileWorldSize);

            int tx = Mathf.FloorToInt(targetPos.x / config.TileWorldSize);

            int ty = Mathf.FloorToInt(targetPos.y / config.TileWorldSize);

            if (!config.IsCoordsValid(sx, sy) || !config.IsCoordsValid(tx, ty))
            {
                return default;
            }

            ushort regStart = gridManager.RawTiles[config.CoordsToIndex(sx, sy)].GeoRegionId;

            ushort regTarget = gridManager.RawTiles[config.CoordsToIndex(tx, ty)].GeoRegionId;

            return FindMacroPathBetweenRegions(regStart, regTarget);
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!drawMacroGraphGizmos || !isInitialized || graph.NodeCount == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            foreach (var kvp in graph.Nodes)
            {
                MacroNavNode n = kvp.Value;

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(n.WorldPositionX, n.WorldPositionY, -2.8f);

                if (n.NodeType == MacroNodeType.RegionCenter)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.2f, 0.8f, 1.0f, 0.7f);

                    Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, 8.0f);
                }
                else
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(1.0f, 0.8f, 0.2f, 0.85f);

                    Gizmos.DrawCube(pos, new Vector3(6.0f, 6.0f, 6.0f));
                }
            }

            if (drawTestedPathGizmo && lastDebugPath.Success && lastDebugPath.Waypoints != null)
            {
                Gizmos.color = Color.magenta;

                for (int i = 0; i < lastDebugPath.Waypoints.Length - 1; i++)
                {
                    Vector3 p1 = new Vector3(lastDebugPath.Waypoints[i].x, lastDebugPath.Waypoints[i].y, -3.2f);

                    Vector3 p2 = new Vector3(lastDebugPath.Waypoints[i + 1].x, lastDebugPath.Waypoints[i + 1].y, -3.2f);

                    Gizmos.DrawLine(p1, p2);

                    Gizmos.DrawSphere(p1, 5.0f);
                }
            }
        }


        private struct BorderPassCandidate
        {
            public ushort RegionA;

            public ushort RegionB;

            public Vector2 WorldPos;

            public float Elevation;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Chunks;
using ER.World.Regions;
using ER.World.Visual;
using ER.World.Inventory;
using ER.Core.Cameras;

namespace ER.World.Polity
{
    public class PolityRegionManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static PolityRegionManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration References")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private ChunkManager chunkManager;

        [SerializeField] private GeoRegionManager regionManager;

        [SerializeField] private InventoryManager inventoryManager;

        [SerializeField] private TilePaletteConfig paletteConfig;


        [Header("Runtime View Mode")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldMapDisplayMode currentDisplayMode = WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes;


        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, PolityData> polities = new Dictionary<ushort, PolityData>();

        private readonly List<PolityAnchorData> anchors = new List<PolityAnchorData>();

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, PolityAnchorData> anchorLookup = new Dictionary<ushort, PolityAnchorData>();

        private ushort nextAnchorId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public IReadOnlyDictionary<ushort, PolityData> Polities => polities;

        public IReadOnlyList<PolityAnchorData> Anchors => anchors;

        public WorldMapDisplayMode CurrentDisplayMode => currentDisplayMode;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (chunkManager == null) chunkManager = ChunkManager.Instance;

            if (regionManager == null) regionManager = GeoRegionManager.Instance;

            if (inventoryManager == null) inventoryManager = InventoryManager.Instance;

            if (regionManager != null)
            {
                regionManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (inventoryManager != null && !inventoryManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                inventoryManager.InitializeStorage();
            }

            if (regionManager == null || !regionManager.IsSegmented)
            {
                return;
            }

            InitializePolitySystem();
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            HandleModeSwitchInput();
        }


        private void HandleModeSwitchInput()
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F1))
            {
                SetDisplayMode(WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F2))
            {
                SetDisplayMode(WorldMapDisplayMode.GeoRegions);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F4))
            {
                SetDisplayMode(WorldMapDisplayMode.PoliticalDeFacto);
            }
            else if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F5))
            {
                SetDisplayMode(WorldMapDisplayMode.PoliticalDeJure);
            }
        }


        public void InitializePolitySystem()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (regionManager == null || !regionManager.IsSegmented || regionManager.Regions == null || regionManager.Regions.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            RegisterDefaultPolities();

            EstablishInitialAnchors();

            if (anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            RecalculateTerritorialControl();

            isInitialized = true;

            RefreshVisuals();

            FocusCameraOnPlayerSettlement();

            Debug.Log($"[PolityRegionManager] Successfully established {anchors.Count} settlements safely in continental heartland.");
        }


        private void RegisterDefaultPolities()
        {
            polities.Clear();

            PolityData playerTribe = PolityData.Create(
                1,
                "РЎРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ Р РѕРґ (РРіСЂРѕРє)",
                new Color32(50, 120, 210, 255),
                12,
                true);

            PolityData rivalTribeA = PolityData.Create(
                2,
                "РЎС‚РµРїРЅС‹Рµ РљРѕС‡РµРІРЅРёРєРё",
                new Color32(210, 75, 60, 255),
                35,
                false);

            PolityData rivalTribeB = PolityData.Create(
                3,
                "Р РµС‡РЅРѕР№ РЎРѕСЋР·",
                new Color32(65, 175, 95, 255),
                60,
                false);

            polities.Add(playerTribe.PolityId, playerTribe);

            polities.Add(rivalTribeA.PolityId, rivalTribeA);

            polities.Add(rivalTribeB.PolityId, rivalTribeB);
        }


        private void EstablishInitialAnchors()
        {
            anchors.Clear();

            anchorLookup.Clear();

            List<GeoRegionData> candidateRegions = FilterViableLandRegions();

            List<Vector2Int> occupiedTiles = new List<Vector2Int>(polities.Count);

            int pIndex = 0;

            foreach (var kvp in polities)
            {
                ushort polityId = kvp.Key;

                Vector2Int anchorTile = SelectBestAnchorLocation(candidateRegions, occupiedTiles, pIndex, polities.Count);

                occupiedTiles.Add(anchorTile);

                int tileIdx = gridManager.MapConfig.CoordsToIndex(anchorTile.x, anchorTile.y);

                ushort geoRegionId = gridManager.RawTiles[tileIdx].GeoRegionId;

                int storageId = 0;

                if (inventoryManager != null)
                {
                    storageId = inventoryManager.CreateInventory(10000.0f, 15000.0f, 60);
                }

                PolityAnchorData anchor = PolityAnchorData.Create(
                    nextAnchorId++,
                    PolityAnchorType.TribalHearth,
                    polityId,
                    geoRegionId,
                    storageId,
                    anchorTile.x,
                    anchorTile.y,
                    radius: 4);

                anchors.Add(anchor);

                anchorLookup.Add(anchor.AnchorId, anchor);

                pIndex++;
            }
        }


        private List<GeoRegionData> FilterViableLandRegions()
        {
            List<GeoRegionData> viable = new List<GeoRegionData>();

            if (regionManager == null || regionManager.Regions == null || gridManager == null)
            {
                return viable;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            float minX = config.MapWidth * 0.18f;

            float maxX = config.MapWidth * 0.82f;

            float minY = config.MapHeight * 0.18f;

            float maxY = config.MapHeight * 0.82f;

            Vector2 mapCenter = new Vector2(config.MapWidth * 0.5f, config.MapHeight * 0.5f);

            var allRegions = regionManager.Regions;

            for (int i = 0; i < allRegions.Count; i++)
            {
                GeoRegionData reg = allRegions[i];

                if (reg.IsOceanRegion || reg.RegionId == 0)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (reg.CenterTileX < minX || reg.CenterTileX > maxX ||
                    reg.CenterTileY < minY || reg.CenterTileY > maxY)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                viable.Add(reg);
            }

            viable.Sort((a, b) =>
            {
                float distA = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(a.CenterTileX, a.CenterTileY), mapCenter);

                float distB = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(b.CenterTileX, b.CenterTileY), mapCenter);

                return distA.CompareTo(distB);
            });

            return viable;
        }


        private Vector2Int SelectBestAnchorLocation(
            List<GeoRegionData> candidateRegions,
            List<Vector2Int> existingAnchors,
            int polityIndex,
            int totalPolities)
        {
            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            float minSeparationTiles = config.MapWidth * 0.12f;

            if (candidateRegions.Count > 0)
            {
                int regionStep = Mathf.Max(1, candidateRegions.Count / totalPolities);

                int startOffset = (polityIndex * regionStep) % candidateRegions.Count;

                for (int attempt = 0; attempt < candidateRegions.Count; attempt++)
                {
                    int rIdx = (startOffset + attempt) % candidateRegions.Count;

                    GeoRegionData candidateReg = candidateRegions[rIdx];

                    if (TryFindBestHabitableTileInRegion(candidateReg, out Vector2Int bestTile))
                    {
                        bool farEnough = true;

                        for (int a = 0; a < existingAnchors.Count; a++)
                        {
                            if (Vector2Int.Distance(bestTile, existingAnchors[a]) < minSeparationTiles)
                            {
                                farEnough = false;

                                break;
                            }
                        }

                        if (farEnough || attempt > candidateRegions.Count / 2)
                        {
                            return bestTile;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            return FindGlobalFallbackLandTile(existingAnchors);
        }


        private bool TryFindBestHabitableTileInRegion(GeoRegionData region, out Vector2Int resultTile)
        {
            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            int minBoundX = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapWidth * 0.15f);

            int maxBoundX = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapWidth * 0.85f);

            int minBoundY = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapHeight * 0.15f);

            int maxBoundY = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapHeight * 0.85f);

            int startX = Mathf.Clamp(region.CenterTileX, minBoundX, maxBoundX);

            int startY = Mathf.Clamp(region.CenterTileY, minBoundY, maxBoundY);

            Vector2Int bestTile = new Vector2Int(startX, startY);

            float bestScore = float.MinValue;

            bool found = false;

            int maxRadius = 48;

            for (int r = 0; r <= maxRadius; r++)
            {
                for (int dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++)
                {
                    for (int dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++)
                    {
                        if (r > 0 && Mathf.Abs(dx) != r && Mathf.Abs(dy) != r) continue;

                        int tx = startX + dx;

                        int ty = startY + dy;

                        if (tx < minBoundX || tx > maxBoundX || ty < minBoundY || ty > maxBoundY) continue;

                        int idx = config.CoordsToIndex(tx, ty);

                        TileData tile = tiles[idx];

                        if (tile.GeoRegionId != region.RegionId) continue;

                        if (!IsTileHabitable(tx, ty, config)) continue;

                        float score = EvaluateTileSettlementScore(tx, ty, config, tiles);

                        score -= (dx * dx + dy * dy) * 0.008f;

                        if (score > bestScore)
                        {
                            bestScore = score;

                            bestTile = new Vector2Int(tx, ty);

                            found = true;
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (found && bestScore >= 45.0f)
                {
                    break;
                }
            }

            resultTile = bestTile;

            return found;
        }


        private float EvaluateTileSettlementScore(
            int tx,
            int ty,
            WorldMapConfig config,
            Unity.Collections.NativeArray<TileData> tiles)
        {
            int idx = config.CoordsToIndex(tx, ty);

            TileData centerTile = tiles[idx];

            float score = 0.0f;

            if (centerTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Plains) score += 40.0f;
            else if (centerTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Hills) score += 25.0f;
            else return -1000.0f;

            if (centerTile.Biome == BiomeType.DenseForest || centerTile.Biome == BiomeType.Taiga) score += 15.0f;
            else if (centerTile.Biome == BiomeType.Steppe) score += 10.0f;

            int habitableNeighbors = 0;

            bool hasWaterNeighbor = false;

            for (int ny = -2; ny <= 2; ny++)
            {
                for (int nx = -2; nx <= 2; nx++)
                {
                    if (nx == 0 && ny == 0) continue;

                    int cx = tx + nx;

                    int cy = ty + ny;

                    if (!config.IsCoordsValid(cx, cy)) continue;

                    int nIdx = config.CoordsToIndex(cx, cy);

                    TileData nTile = tiles[nIdx];

                    if (nTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Plains || nTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Hills)
                    {
                        habitableNeighbors++;
                    }
                    else if (nTile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterShallow)
                    {
                        hasWaterNeighbor = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            score += habitableNeighbors * 2.5f;

            if (hasWaterNeighbor) score += 20.0f;

            return score;
        }


        private Vector2Int FindGlobalFallbackLandTile(List<Vector2Int> existingAnchors)
        {
            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            int cx = config.MapWidth / 2;

            int cy = config.MapHeight / 2;

            Vector2Int fallback = new Vector2Int(cx, cy);

            float bestScore = float.MinValue;

            int minBoundX = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapWidth * 0.15f);

            int maxBoundX = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapWidth * 0.85f);

            int minBoundY = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapHeight * 0.15f);

            int maxBoundY = Mathf.RoundToInt(config.MapHeight * 0.85f);

            for (int step = 10; step < config.MapWidth * 0.35f; step += 15)
            {
                for (int signY = -1; signY <= 1; signY += 2)
                {
                    for (int signX = -1; signX <= 1; signX += 2)
                    {
                        int tx = cx + step * signX;

                        int ty = cy + step * signY;

                        if (tx < minBoundX || tx > maxBoundX || ty < minBoundY || ty > maxBoundY) continue;

                        if (IsTileHabitable(tx, ty, config))
                        {
                            float score = EvaluateTileSettlementScore(tx, ty, config, tiles);

                            for (int a = 0; a < existingAnchors.Count; a++)
                            {
                                score += Vector2Int.Distance(new Vector2Int(tx, ty), existingAnchors[a]) * 0.1f;
                            }

                            if (score > bestScore)
                            {
                                bestScore = score;

                                fallback = new Vector2Int(tx, ty);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (bestScore > 35.0f)
                {
                    return fallback;
                }
            }

            return fallback;
        }


        private bool IsTileHabitable(int tileX, int tileY, WorldMapConfig config)
        {
            if (!config.IsCoordsValid(tileX, tileY)) return false;

            int idx = config.CoordsToIndex(tileX, tileY);

            TileData tile = gridManager.RawTiles[idx];

            return tile.Terrain != TerrainType.WaterDeep &&
                   tile.Terrain != TerrainType.WaterShallow &&
                   tile.Terrain != TerrainType.Mountains;
        }


        private void FocusCameraOnPlayerSettlement()
        {
            if (anchors.Count == 0 || gridManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            StrategicCameraController cam = StrategicCameraController.Instance;

            if (cam == null)
            {
                cam = FindFirstObjectByType<StrategicCameraController>();
            }

            if (cam != null)
            {
                PolityAnchorData playerAnchor = anchors[0];

                float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

                float hqX = (playerAnchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float hqY = (playerAnchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                cam.FocusOnCoordinates(hqX, hqY, 160.0f);
            }
        }


        public bool TryGetAnchor(ushort anchorId, out PolityAnchorData anchor)
        {
            return anchorLookup.TryGetValue(anchorId, out anchor);
        }


        public void RecalculateTerritorialControl()
        {
            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            int totalTiles = tiles.Length;

            for (int i = 0; i < totalTiles; i++)
            {
                TileData tile = tiles[i];

                tile.DeFactoPolityId = 0;

                tile.DeJurePolityId = 0;

                tiles[i] = tile;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            for (int a = 0; a < anchors.Count; a++)
            {
                PolityAnchorData anchor = anchors[a];

                if (!anchor.IsActive)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                ProjectAnchorInfluence(anchor, tiles, config);
            }
        }


        private void ProjectAnchorInfluence(
            PolityAnchorData anchor,
            Unity.Collections.NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig config)
        {
            int centerX = anchor.TileX;

            int centerY = anchor.TileY;

            int radius = anchor.InfluenceRadius;

            int radiusSqr = radius * radius;

            for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++)
            {
                int targetY = centerY + dy;

                if (targetY < 0 || targetY >= config.MapHeight)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++)
                {
                    int targetX = centerX + dx;

                    if (targetX < 0 || targetX >= config.MapWidth)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (dx * dx + dy * dy > radiusSqr)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    int tileIndex = config.CoordsToIndex(targetX, targetY);

                    TileData tile = tiles[tileIndex];

                    if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains || tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    tile.DeFactoPolityId = anchor.OwnerPolityId;

                    tile.DeJurePolityId = anchor.OwnerPolityId;

                    tiles[tileIndex] = tile;
                }
            }
        }


        public void PaintTileControl(int centerX, int centerY, ushort polityId, int brushRadius, bool setDeFacto = true, bool setDeJure = true)
        {
            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int radiusSqr = brushRadius * brushRadius;

            for (int dy = -brushRadius; dy <= brushRadius; dy++)
            {
                int y = centerY + dy;

                if (y < 0 || y >= config.MapHeight) continue;

                for (int dx = -brushRadius; dx <= brushRadius; dx++)
                {
                    int x = centerX + dx;

                    if (x < 0 || x >= config.MapWidth) continue;

                    if (dx * dx + dy * dy > radiusSqr) continue;

                    int index = config.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

                    TileData tile = tiles[index];

                    if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains || tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (setDeFacto) tile.DeFactoPolityId = polityId;

                    if (setDeJure) tile.DeJurePolityId = polityId;

                    tiles[index] = tile;
                }
            }

            RefreshVisuals();
        }


        public void FillRegionControl(ushort geoRegionId, ushort polityId, bool setDeFacto = true, bool setDeJure = true)
        {
            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized || geoRegionId == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            var tiles = gridManager.RawTiles;

            int totalTiles = tiles.Length;

            for (int i = 0; i < totalTiles; i++)
            {
                TileData tile = tiles[i];

                if (tile.GeoRegionId == geoRegionId)
                {
                    if (tile.Terrain != TerrainType.Mountains && tile.Terrain != TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                    {
                        if (setDeFacto) tile.DeFactoPolityId = polityId;

                        if (setDeJure) tile.DeJurePolityId = polityId;

                        tiles[i] = tile;
                    }
                }
            }

            RefreshVisuals();
        }


        public void SetDisplayMode(WorldMapDisplayMode mode)
        {
            currentDisplayMode = mode;

            RefreshVisuals();
        }


        public void RefreshVisuals()
        {
            if (chunkManager == null || !chunkManager.IsInitialized || paletteConfig == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            WorldChunk[,] chunks = chunkManager.Chunks;

            int countX = chunks.GetLength(0);

            int countY = chunks.GetLength(1);

            for (int y = 0; y < countY; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < countX; x++)
                {
                    chunks[x, y].RefreshChunkColors(gridManager.RawTiles, gridManager.MapConfig, paletteConfig, currentDisplayMode, polities);
                }
            }
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (anchors == null || gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            for (int i = 0; i < anchors.Count; i++)
            {
                PolityAnchorData anchor = anchors[i];

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3((anchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize, (anchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize, -2.0f);

                if (polities.TryGetValue(anchor.OwnerPolityId, out PolityData polity))
                {
                    Gizmos.color = (Color)polity.MapColor;
                }
                else
                {
                    Gizmos.color = Color.white;
                }

                Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, tileSize * 0.4f);

                Gizmos.color = Color.white;

                Gizmos.DrawWireSphere(pos, anchor.InfluenceRadius * tileSize);
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Regions\GeoRegionAdjacencyBuilder.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;

namespace ER.World.Regions
{
    public static class GeoRegionAdjacencyBuilder
    {
        private class EdgeAccumulator
        {
            public ushort RegionA;

            public ushort RegionB;

            public int ContactCount;

            public int BestPassXA;

            public int BestPassYA;

            public int BestPassXB;

            public int BestPassYB;

            public int LowestCost;

            public byte LowestElevation;

            public bool IsOcean;
        }


        public static MacroAdjacencyGraph BuildAdjacencyGraph(NativeArray<TileData> tiles, WorldMapConfig mapConfig)
        {
            MacroAdjacencyGraph graph = new MacroAdjacencyGraph();

            int width = mapConfig.MapWidth;

            int height = mapConfig.MapHeight;

            Dictionary<long, EdgeAccumulator> edgeMap = new Dictionary<long, EdgeAccumulator>(512);

            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
                {
                    int currentIndex = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

                    TileData currentTile = tiles[currentIndex];

                    ushort regionCurrent = currentTile.GeoRegionId;

                    if (regionCurrent == 0)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (x + 1 < width)
                    {
                        ProcessNeighborPair(x, y, x + 1, y, currentTile, tiles, mapConfig, edgeMap);
                    }

                    if (y + 1 < height)
                    {
                        ProcessNeighborPair(x, y, x, y + 1, currentTile, tiles, mapConfig, edgeMap);
                    }
                }
            }

            foreach (var pair in edgeMap.Values)
            {
                GeoRegionEdge edge = new GeoRegionEdge
                {
                    RegionA = pair.RegionA,
                    RegionB = pair.RegionB,
                    ContactTilesCount = pair.ContactCount,
                    PassTileXA = pair.BestPassXA,
                    PassTileYA = pair.BestPassYA,
                    PassTileXB = pair.BestPassXB,
                    PassTileYB = pair.BestPassYB,
                    BestPassElevation = pair.LowestElevation,
                    MinTransitCost = pair.LowestCost,
                    IsOceanConnection = pair.IsOcean
                };

                graph.AddOrUpdateEdge(edge);
            }

            return graph;
        }


        private static void ProcessNeighborPair(
            int xA, int yA,
            int xB, int yB,
            TileData tileA,
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig mapConfig,
            Dictionary<long, EdgeAccumulator> edgeMap)
        {
            int indexB = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(xB, yB);

            TileData tileB = tiles[indexB];

            ushort regA = tileA.GeoRegionId;

            ushort regB = tileB.GeoRegionId;

            if (regB == 0 || regA == regB)
            {
                return;
            }

            long edgeKey = MacroAdjacencyGraph.MakeEdgeKey(regA, regB);

            int pairCost = (tileA.MovementCost + tileB.MovementCost) / 2 + Math.Abs(tileA.Elevation - tileB.Elevation) * 3;

            byte maxElevation = (byte)Math.Max(tileA.Elevation, tileB.Elevation);

            bool isOceanEdge = tileA.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep && tileB.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep;

            if (!edgeMap.TryGetValue(edgeKey, out EdgeAccumulator accum))
            {
                accum = new EdgeAccumulator
                {
                    RegionA = regA < regB ? regA : regB,
                    RegionB = regA < regB ? regB : regA,
                    ContactCount = 1,
                    BestPassXA = xA,
                    BestPassYA = yA,
                    BestPassXB = xB,
                    BestPassYB = yB,
                    LowestCost = pairCost,
                    LowestElevation = maxElevation,
                    IsOcean = isOceanEdge
                };

                edgeMap[edgeKey] = accum;
            }
            else
            {
                accum.ContactCount++;

                if (pairCost < accum.LowestCost)
                {
                    accum.LowestCost = pairCost;

                    accum.LowestElevation = maxElevation;

                    accum.BestPassXA = xA;

                    accum.BestPassYA = yA;

                    accum.BestPassXB = xB;

                    accum.BestPassYB = yB;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Regions\GeoRegionEdge.cs ===
using System;

namespace ER.World.Regions
{
    [Serializable]
    public struct GeoRegionEdge
    {
        public ushort RegionA;

        public ushort RegionB;

        public int ContactTilesCount;

        public int PassTileXA;

        public int PassTileYA;

        public int PassTileXB;

        public int PassTileYB;

        public byte BestPassElevation;

        public int MinTransitCost;

        public bool IsOceanConnection;


        public ushort GetOppositeRegion(ushort regionId)
        {
            if (regionId == RegionA)
            {
                return RegionB;
            }

            if (regionId == RegionB)
            {
                return RegionA;
            }

            return 0;
        }
    }
}
