using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Chunks;
using ER.World.Visual;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Regions
{
    public class GeoRegionManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static GeoRegionManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private ChunkManager chunkManager;

        [SerializeField] private TilePaletteConfig paletteConfig;

        [SerializeField] private int targetRegionCount = 210;


        [Header("Debug Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool showRegionBoundariesView = false;

        [SerializeField] private bool drawAdjacencyGraphGizmos = true;


        private GeoRegionData[] regions;

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, GeoRegionData> regionLookup = new Dictionary<ushort, GeoRegionData>();

        private MacroAdjacencyGraph adjacencyGraph;

        private bool isSegmented = false;


        public bool IsSegmented => isSegmented;

        public IReadOnlyList<GeoRegionData> Regions => regions;

        public MacroAdjacencyGraph AdjacencyGraph => adjacencyGraph;

        public bool ShowRegionBoundariesView => showRegionBoundariesView;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isSegmented)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null)
            {
                gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;
            }

            if (chunkManager == null)
            {
                chunkManager = ChunkManager.Instance;
            }

            if (gridManager != null && !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                gridManager.InitializeGrid();
            }

            if (gridManager != null && gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                ExecuteSegmentation();
            }
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.F2))
            {
                ToggleRegionViewMode();
            }
        }


        public void ExecuteSegmentation()
        {
            if (isSegmented || gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            regions = GeoRegionSegmentation.GenerateRegions(gridManager.RawTiles, gridManager.MapConfig, targetRegionCount, 30);

            regionLookup.Clear();

            for (int i = 0; i < regions.Length; i++)
            {
                regionLookup[regions[i].RegionId] = regions[i];
            }

            adjacencyGraph = GeoRegionAdjacencyBuilder.BuildAdjacencyGraph(gridManager.RawTiles, gridManager.MapConfig);

            isSegmented = true;
        }


        public bool TryGetRegion(ushort regionId, out GeoRegionData regionData)
        {
            return regionLookup.TryGetValue(regionId, out regionData);
        }


        public bool TryGetRegionAt(int tileX, int tileY, out GeoRegionData regionData)
        {
            if (gridManager != null && gridManager.TryGetTile(tileX, tileY, out TileData tile))
            {
                return TryGetRegion(tile.GeoRegionId, out regionData);
            }

            regionData = default;

            return false;
        }


        public void ToggleRegionViewMode()
        {
            showRegionBoundariesView = !showRegionBoundariesView;

            if (PolityRegionManager.Instance != null)
            {
                WorldMapDisplayMode targetMode = showRegionBoundariesView ? WorldMapDisplayMode.GeoRegions : WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes;

                PolityRegionManager.Instance.SetDisplayMode(targetMode);

                return;
            }

            if (chunkManager != null && chunkManager.IsInitialized && paletteConfig != null)
            {
                WorldChunk[,] chunks = chunkManager.Chunks;

                int countX = chunks.GetLength(0);

                int countY = chunks.GetLength(1);

                WorldMapDisplayMode mode = showRegionBoundariesView ? WorldMapDisplayMode.GeoRegions : WorldMapDisplayMode.Biomes;

                for (int y = 0; y < countY; y++)
                {
                    for (int x = 0; x < countX; x++)
                    {
                        chunks[x, y].RefreshChunkColors(gridManager.RawTiles, gridManager.MapConfig, paletteConfig, mode);
                    }
                }
            }
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmosSelected()
        {
            if (!drawAdjacencyGraphGizmos || adjacencyGraph == null || !isSegmented || gridManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            IReadOnlyList<GeoRegionEdge> edges = adjacencyGraph.Edges;

            for (int i = 0; i < edges.Count; i++)
            {
                GeoRegionEdge edge = edges[i];

                if (!regionLookup.TryGetValue(edge.RegionA, out GeoRegionData regA)) continue;

                if (!regionLookup.TryGetValue(edge.RegionB, out GeoRegionData regB)) continue;

                Vector3 posA = new Vector3(regA.CenterTileX * tileSize, regA.CenterTileY * tileSize, -1.0f);

                Vector3 posB = new Vector3(regB.CenterTileX * tileSize, regB.CenterTileY * tileSize, -1.0f);

                Vector3 passPos = new Vector3(edge.PassTileXA * tileSize, edge.PassTileYA * tileSize, -2.0f);

                Gizmos.color = edge.IsOceanConnection ? new Color(0.2f, 0.6f, 0.9f, 0.35f) : new Color(0.9f, 0.7f, 0.2f, 0.45f);

                Gizmos.DrawLine(posA, passPos);

                Gizmos.DrawLine(passPos, posB);

                Gizmos.color = Color.red;

                Gizmos.DrawWireSphere(passPos, tileSize * 1.5f);
            }
        }
    }

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Regions\GeoRegionSegmentation.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;

namespace ER.World.Regions
{
    public static class GeoRegionSegmentation
    {
        private struct HeapNode
        {
            public int TileIndex;

            public int Cost;
        }


        private class FastMinHeap
        {
            private HeapNode[] elements;

            private int count;


            public int Count => count;


            public FastMinHeap(int capacity)
            {
                elements = new HeapNode[capacity];

                count = 0;
            }


            public void Push(int tileIndex, int cost)
            {
                if (count >= elements.Length)
                {
                    Array.Resize(ref elements, elements.Length * 2);
                }

                elements[count] = new HeapNode { TileIndex = tileIndex, Cost = cost };

                BubbleUp(count);

                count++;
            }


            public HeapNode Pop()
            {
                HeapNode result = elements[0];

                count--;

                if (count > 0)
                {
                    elements[0] = elements[count];

                    BubbleDown(0);
                }

                return result;
            }


            private void BubbleUp(int index)
            {
                while (index > 0)
                {
                    int parent = (index - 1) / 2;

                    if (elements[index].Cost >= elements[parent].Cost)
                    {
                        break;
                    }

                    Swap(index, parent);

                    index = parent;
                }
            }


            private void BubbleDown(int index)
            {
                while (true)
                {
                    int left = 2 * index + 1;

                    int right = 2 * index + 2;

                    int smallest = index;

                    if (left < count && elements[left].Cost < elements[smallest].Cost)
                    {
                        smallest = left;
                    }

                    if (right < count && elements[right].Cost < elements[smallest].Cost)
                    {
                        smallest = right;
                    }

                    if (smallest == index)
                    {
                        break;
                    }

                    Swap(index, smallest);

                    index = smallest;
                }
            }


            private void Swap(int a, int b)
            {
                HeapNode temp = elements[a];

                elements[a] = elements[b];

                elements[b] = temp;
            }
        }


        private struct RegionSeed
        {
            public ushort Id;

            public int TileIndex;

            public bool IsOcean;

            public int X;

            public int Y;
        }


        public static GeoRegionData[] GenerateRegions(
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig mapConfig,
            int targetLandRegionsCount = 180,
            int targetOceanRegionsCount = 30)
        {
            int totalTiles = mapConfig.TotalTilesCount;

            ushort[] regionAssignments = new ushort[totalTiles];

            int[] costToReach = new int[totalTiles];

            for (int i = 0; i < totalTiles; i++)
            {
                costToReach[i] = int.MaxValue;
            }

            List<RegionSeed> allSeeds = DistributeTopographicalSeeds(tiles, mapConfig, targetLandRegionsCount, targetOceanRegionsCount);

            FastMinHeap heap = new FastMinHeap(allSeeds.Count * 256);

            for (int i = 0; i < allSeeds.Count; i++)
            {
                RegionSeed seed = allSeeds[i];

                regionAssignments[seed.TileIndex] = seed.Id;

                costToReach[seed.TileIndex] = 0;

                heap.Push(seed.TileIndex, 0);
            }

            int[] neighborOffsetsX = { 0, 0, -1, 1 };

            int[] neighborOffsetsY = { -1, 1, 0, 0 };

            while (heap.Count > 0)
            {
                HeapNode currentNode = heap.Pop();

                int currentIndex = currentNode.TileIndex;

                if (currentNode.Cost > costToReach[currentIndex])
                {
                    continue;
                }

                ushort currentRegionId = regionAssignments[currentIndex];

                bool isCurrentOcean = currentRegionId > targetLandRegionsCount;

                mapConfig.IndexToCoords(currentIndex, out int curX, out int curY);

                TileData currentTile = tiles[currentIndex];

                for (int dir = 0; dir < 4; dir++)
                {
                    int neighborX = curX + neighborOffsetsX[dir];

                    int neighborY = curY + neighborOffsetsY[dir];

                    if (!mapConfig.IsCoordsValid(neighborX, neighborY))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    int neighborIndex = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(neighborX, neighborY);

                    TileData neighborTile = tiles[neighborIndex];

                    bool isNeighborOcean = neighborTile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep;

                    if (isCurrentOcean != isNeighborOcean)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (!isCurrentOcean && neighborTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Mountains)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    int traversalWeight = CalculateTopographicalWeight(currentTile, neighborTile, isCurrentOcean);

                    int newTotalCost = currentNode.Cost + traversalWeight;

                    if (newTotalCost < costToReach[neighborIndex])
                    {
                        costToReach[neighborIndex] = newTotalCost;

                        regionAssignments[neighborIndex] = currentRegionId;

                        heap.Push(neighborIndex, newTotalCost);
                    }
                }
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < totalTiles; i++)
            {
                TileData tile = tiles[i];

                tile.GeoRegionId = regionAssignments[i];

                tiles[i] = tile;
            }

            return CompileRegionData(tiles, mapConfig, allSeeds, regionAssignments);
        }


        private static int CalculateTopographicalWeight(TileData fromTile, TileData toTile, bool isOcean)
        {
            if (isOcean)
            {
                return 15;
            }

            int baseCost = 8;

            int elevationFactor = toTile.Elevation / 16;

            int elevationBarrier = elevationFactor * elevationFactor;

            int climbPenalty = 0;

            if (toTile.Elevation > fromTile.Elevation)
            {
                climbPenalty = (toTile.Elevation - fromTile.Elevation) * 4;
            }

            int terrainPenalty = 0;

            if (toTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Hills)
            {
                terrainPenalty = 55;
            }
            else if (toTile.Terrain == TerrainType.Swamp)
            {
                terrainPenalty = 60;
            }
            else if (toTile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterShallow)
            {
                terrainPenalty = 90;
            }

            return baseCost + elevationBarrier + climbPenalty + terrainPenalty;
        }


        private static List<RegionSeed> DistributeTopographicalSeeds(
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig mapConfig,
            int landTarget,
            int oceanTarget)
        {
            List<RegionSeed> seeds = new List<RegionSeed>(landTarget + oceanTarget);

            List<int> landCandidates = new List<int>(10000);

            List<int> oceanCandidates = new List<int>(5000);

            int width = mapConfig.MapWidth;

            int height = mapConfig.MapHeight;

            int step = 6;

            for (int y = step; y < height - step; y += step)
            {
                for (int x = step; x < width - step; x += step)
                {
                    int index = mapConfig.CoordsToIndex(x, y);

                    TileData tile = tiles[index];

                    if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.Plains && tile.Elevation < 135)
                    {
                        landCandidates.Add(index);
                    }
                    else if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
                    {
                        oceanCandidates.Add(index);
                    }
                }
            }

            ushort regionIdCounter = 1;

            SelectFarthestSeeds(landCandidates, seeds, landTarget, ref regionIdCounter, false, mapConfig);

            SelectFarthestSeeds(oceanCandidates, seeds, oceanTarget, ref regionIdCounter, true, mapConfig);

            return seeds;
        }


        private static void SelectFarthestSeeds(
            List<int> candidates,
            List<RegionSeed> outSeeds,
            int count,
            ref ushort idCounter,
            bool isOcean,
            WorldMapConfig mapConfig)
        {
            if (candidates.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            List<int> selectedIndices = new List<int>(count);

            int firstIndex = candidates[UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, candidates.Count)];

            selectedIndices.Add(firstIndex);

            mapConfig.IndexToCoords(firstIndex, out int fx, out int fy);

            outSeeds.Add(new RegionSeed
            {
                Id = idCounter++,
                TileIndex = firstIndex,
                IsOcean = isOcean,
                X = fx,
                Y = fy
            });

            for (int i = 1; i < count; i++)
            {
                int bestCandidate = -1;

                long maxMinDistance = -1;

                for (int c = 0; c < candidates.Count; c++)
                {
                    int candidateTile = candidates[c];

                    mapConfig.IndexToCoords(candidateTile, out int cx, out int cy);

                    long minDistanceToSelected = long.MaxValue;

                    for (int s = 0; s < selectedIndices.Count; s++)
                    {
                        mapConfig.IndexToCoords(selectedIndices[s], out int sx, out int sy);

                        long dx = cx - sx;

                        long dy = cy - sy;

                        long distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy;

                        if (distSqr < minDistanceToSelected)
                        {
                            minDistanceToSelected = distSqr;
                        }
                    }

                    if (minDistanceToSelected > maxMinDistance)
                    {
                        maxMinDistance = minDistanceToSelected;

                        bestCandidate = candidateTile;
                    }
                }

                if (bestCandidate >= 0)
                {
                    selectedIndices.Add(bestCandidate);

                    mapConfig.IndexToCoords(bestCandidate, out int bx, out int by);

                    outSeeds.Add(new RegionSeed
                    {
                        Id = idCounter++,
                        TileIndex = bestCandidate,
                        IsOcean = isOcean,
                        X = bx,
                        Y = by
                    });
                }
            }
        }


        private static GeoRegionData[] CompileRegionData(
            NativeArray<TileData> tiles,
            WorldMapConfig mapConfig,
            List<RegionSeed> seeds,
            ushort[] regionAssignments)
        {
            int count = seeds.Count;

            Dictionary<ushort, GeoRegionData> map = new Dictionary<ushort, GeoRegionData>(count);

            Dictionary<ushort, long> elevationSums = new Dictionary<ushort, long>(count);

            Dictionary<ushort, long> moistureSums = new Dictionary<ushort, long>(count);

            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                ushort id = seeds[i].Id;

                map[id] = GeoRegionData.CreateDefault(id, seeds[i].IsOcean);

                elevationSums[id] = 0;

                moistureSums[id] = 0;
            }

            int totalTiles = mapConfig.TotalTilesCount;

            for (int i = 0; i < totalTiles; i++)
            {
                ushort regId = regionAssignments[i];

                if (regId == 0 || !map.ContainsKey(regId))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                mapConfig.IndexToCoords(i, out int x, out int y);

                TileData t = tiles[i];

                GeoRegionData data = map[regId];

                data.TotalTilesCount++;

                if (x < data.MinTileX) data.MinTileX = x;

                if (x > data.MaxTileX) data.MaxTileX = x;

                if (y < data.MinTileY) data.MinTileY = y;

                if (y > data.MaxTileY) data.MaxTileY = y;

                map[regId] = data;

                elevationSums[regId] += t.Elevation;

                moistureSums[regId] += t.Moisture;
            }

            GeoRegionData[] finalRegions = new GeoRegionData[count];

            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                RegionSeed s = seeds[i];

                GeoRegionData data = map[s.Id];

                data.CenterTileX = s.X;

                data.CenterTileY = s.Y;

                if (data.TotalTilesCount > 0)
                {
                    data.AverageElevation = (byte)(elevationSums[s.Id] / data.TotalTilesCount);

                    data.AverageMoisture = (byte)(moistureSums[s.Id] / data.TotalTilesCount);
                }

                finalRegions[i] = data;
            }

            return finalRegions;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Regions\MacroAdjacencyGraph.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ER.World.Regions
{
    [Serializable]
    public class MacroAdjacencyGraph
    {
        private readonly List<GeoRegionEdge> edges = new List<GeoRegionEdge>();

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, List<int>> adjacencyLookup = new Dictionary<ushort, List<int>>();

        private readonly Dictionary<long, int> edgeKeyToEdgeIndex = new Dictionary<long, int>();


        public IReadOnlyList<GeoRegionEdge> Edges => edges;

        public int TotalEdgesCount => edges.Count;


        public void Clear()
        {
            edges.Clear();

            adjacencyLookup.Clear();

            edgeKeyToEdgeIndex.Clear();
        }


        public void AddOrUpdateEdge(GeoRegionEdge edge)
        {
            long key = MakeEdgeKey(edge.RegionA, edge.RegionB);

            if (edgeKeyToEdgeIndex.TryGetValue(key, out int existingIndex))
            {
                edges[existingIndex] = edge;

                return;
            }

            int newIndex = edges.Count;

            edges.Add(edge);

            edgeKeyToEdgeIndex[key] = newIndex;

            RegisterInLookup(edge.RegionA, newIndex);

            RegisterInLookup(edge.RegionB, newIndex);
        }


        private void RegisterInLookup(ushort regionId, int edgeIndex)
        {
            if (!adjacencyLookup.TryGetValue(regionId, out List<int> list))
            {
                list = new List<int>(8);

                adjacencyLookup[regionId] = list;
            }

            list.Add(edgeIndex);
        }


        public bool TryGetEdge(ushort regionA, ushort regionB, out GeoRegionEdge edge)
        {
            long key = MakeEdgeKey(regionA, regionB);

            if (edgeKeyToEdgeIndex.TryGetValue(key, out int edgeIndex))
            {
                edge = edges[edgeIndex];

                return true;
            }

            edge = default;

            return false;
        }


        public bool AreAdjacent(ushort regionA, ushort regionB)
        {
            long key = MakeEdgeKey(regionA, regionB);

            return edgeKeyToEdgeIndex.ContainsKey(key);
        }


        public int GetConnectedEdges(ushort regionId, List<GeoRegionEdge> results)
        {
            if (results == null)
            {
                return 0;
            }

            results.Clear();

            if (!adjacencyLookup.TryGetValue(regionId, out List<int> edgeIndices))
            {
                return 0;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < edgeIndices.Count; i++)
            {
                results.Add(edges[edgeIndices[i]]);
            }

            return results.Count;
        }


        public static long MakeEdgeKey(ushort a, ushort b)
        {
            ushort min = a < b ? a : b;

            ushort max = a < b ? b : a;

            return ((long)min << 32) | (long)max;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\03_World\Resources\WorldResourceManager.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Polity;
using ER.Simulation.Time;

namespace ER.World.Resources
{
    public class WorldResourceManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static WorldResourceManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Spawn Configuration")]
        [SerializeField] private int nodesPerSettlement = 40;

        [SerializeField] private float minSpawnRadius = 8.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float maxSpawnRadius = 38.0f;


        [Header("References")]
        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;


        [Header("Debug Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool drawResourceGizmos = true;


        private NativeList<WorldResourceNodeData> resourceNodes;

        private int nextNodeId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public NativeList<WorldResourceNodeData> RawNodes => resourceNodes;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeStorage();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (clockManager == null) clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager != null)
            {
                polityManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (polityManager == null || !polityManager.IsInitialized || polityManager.Anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            SpawnInitialResources();

            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed += HandleDayPassed;
            }

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void InitializeStorage()
        {
            if (!resourceNodes.IsCreated)
            {
                resourceNodes = new NativeList<WorldResourceNodeData>(2048, Allocator.Persistent);
            }
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed -= HandleDayPassed;
            }

            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void DisposeStorage()
        {
            if (resourceNodes.IsCreated)
            {
                resourceNodes.Dispose();
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        private void SpawnInitialResources()
        {
            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            for (int a = 0; a < anchors.Count; a++)
            {
                PolityAnchorData anchor = anchors[a];

                float centerX = (anchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float centerY = (anchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                for (int i = 0; i < nodesPerSettlement; i++)
                {
                    Vector2 offset = UnityEngine.Random.insideUnitCircle.normalized * UnityEngine.Random.Range(minSpawnRadius, maxSpawnRadius);

                    float posX = centerX + offset.x;

                    float posY = centerY + offset.y;

                    if (!ValidatePlacement(posX, posY))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    int roll = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, 100);

                    if (roll < 45)
                    {
                        SpawnNode(ResourceNodeType.FallenTree, 1, 0, posX, posY, (uint)UnityEngine.Random.Range(20, 50), 1.0f);
                    }
                    else if (roll < 75)
                    {
                        SpawnNode(ResourceNodeType.FlintDeposit, 2, 0, posX, posY, (uint)UnityEngine.Random.Range(15, 35), 1.5f);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SpawnNode(ResourceNodeType.BerryBush, 3, 0, posX, posY, (uint)UnityEngine.Random.Range(40, 80), 0.5f);
                    }
                }
            }

            Debug.Log($"[WorldResourceManager] Successfully spawned {resourceNodes.Length} resource nodes in the wild.");
        }


        private bool ValidatePlacement(float worldX, float worldY)
        {
            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized) return false;

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int tx = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldX / config.TileWorldSize);

            int ty = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldY / config.TileWorldSize);

            if (!config.IsCoordsValid(tx, ty)) return false;

            TileData tile = gridManager.GetTile(tx, ty);

            return tile.Terrain != TerrainType.WaterDeep && tile.Terrain != TerrainType.Mountains;
        }


        public int SpawnNode(ResourceNodeType type, ushort itemId, ushort toolId, float x, float y, uint yieldAmount, float hardness)
        {
            int id = nextNodeId++;

            WorldResourceNodeData node = WorldResourceNodeData.Create(id, type, itemId, toolId, x, y, yieldAmount, hardness);

            resourceNodes.Add(node);

            return id;
        }


        public bool TryGetNode(int nodeId, out WorldResourceNodeData node, out int arrayIndex)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < resourceNodes.Length; i++)
            {
                if (resourceNodes[i].NodeId == nodeId && !resourceNodes[i].IsDepleted)
                {
                    node = resourceNodes[i];

                    arrayIndex = i;

                    return true;
                }
            }

            node = default;

            arrayIndex = -1;

            return false;
        }


        public void SetNode(int index, WorldResourceNodeData node)
        {
            if (index >= 0 && index < resourceNodes.Length)
            {
                resourceNodes[index] = node;
            }
        }


        private void HandleDayPassed(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            if (!resourceNodes.IsCreated || timeData.CurrentSeason == Season.Winter)
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < resourceNodes.Length; i++)
            {
                WorldResourceNodeData node = resourceNodes[i];

                if (node.NodeType == ResourceNodeType.BerryBush)
                {
                    if (node.IsDepleted)
                    {
                        node.IsDepleted = false;

                        node.RemainingYield = 10;
                    }
                    else if (node.RemainingYield < 80)
                    {
                        node.RemainingYield = System.Math.Min(80u, node.RemainingYield + 4u);
                    }

                    resourceNodes[i] = node;
                }
            }
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!drawResourceGizmos || !resourceNodes.IsCreated || resourceNodes.Length == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < resourceNodes.Length; i++)
            {
                WorldResourceNodeData node = resourceNodes[i];

                if (node.IsDepleted) continue;

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(node.WorldPositionX, node.WorldPositionY, -2.2f);

                switch (node.NodeType)
                {
                    case ResourceNodeType.FallenTree:
                        Gizmos.color = new Color(0.6f, 0.4f, 0.2f);
                        Gizmos.DrawCube(pos, new Vector3(1.4f, 0.6f, 0.6f));
                        break;

                    case ResourceNodeType.FlintDeposit:
                        Gizmos.color = new Color(0.5f, 0.55f, 0.6f);
                        Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, 0.7f);
                        break;

                    case ResourceNodeType.BerryBush:
                        Gizmos.color = new Color(0.85f, 0.15f, 0.35f);
                        Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, 0.6f);
                        break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

    {

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct WorldResourceNodeData
    {

        public ResourceNodeType NodeType;

        public ushort ItemTypeId;

        public ushort RequiredToolTypeId;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public uint RemainingYield;

        public float Hardness;

        public bool IsDepleted;

            int id,
            ResourceNodeType type,
            ushort itemTypeId,
            ushort toolTypeId,
            float x,
            float y,
            uint yieldAmount,
            float hardness = 1.0f)
        {

            {
                NodeId = id,
                NodeType = type,
                ItemTypeId = itemTypeId,
                RequiredToolTypeId = toolTypeId,
                WorldPositionX = x,
                WorldPositionY = y,
                RemainingYield = yieldAmount,
                Hardness = hardness,
                IsDepleted = false
            };
        }
    }

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "ItemDatabaseConfig", menuName = "ER/04_Inventory/Item Database Config")]
    public class ItemDatabaseConfig : ScriptableObject
    {
        [Header("Item Definitions")]
        [SerializeField] private List<ItemTypeConfig> itemTypes = new List<ItemTypeConfig>();


        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, ItemTypeConfig> lookup = new Dictionary<ushort, ItemTypeConfig>();

        private bool isLookupBuilt = false;


        public IReadOnlyList<ItemTypeConfig> ItemTypes => itemTypes;


        public void BuildLookup()
        {
            lookup.Clear();

            for (int i = 0; i < itemTypes.Count; i++)
            {
                ItemTypeConfig item = itemTypes[i];

                lookup[item.ItemTypeId] = item;
            }

            isLookupBuilt = true;
        }


        public bool TryGetItemConfig(ushort itemTypeId, out ItemTypeConfig config)
        {
            if (!isLookupBuilt)
            {
                BuildLookup();
            }

            return lookup.TryGetValue(itemTypeId, out config);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Data\InventoryHeaderData.cs ===
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct InventoryHeaderData
    {
        public int InventoryId;

        public float MaxWeightKg;

        public float MaxVolumeLiters;

        public float CurrentWeightKg;

        public float CurrentVolumeLiters;

        public byte ProtectionLevel;

        public byte Flags;

        public ushort SlotCount;


        public const byte FlagIsLocked = 1 << 0;

        public const byte FlagAllowOverflow = 1 << 1;


        public static InventoryHeaderData Create(
            int inventoryId,
            float maxWeight,
            float maxVolume,
            byte protection = 128)
        {
            return new InventoryHeaderData
            {
                InventoryId = inventoryId,
                MaxWeightKg = maxWeight,
                MaxVolumeLiters = maxVolume,
                CurrentWeightKg = 0.0f,
                CurrentVolumeLiters = 0.0f,
                ProtectionLevel = protection,
                Flags = 0,
                SlotCount = 0
            };
        }


        public bool CanAccommodate(float addedWeight, float addedVolume)
        {
            if ((Flags & FlagAllowOverflow) != 0)
            {
                return true;
            }

            return (CurrentWeightKg + addedWeight <= MaxWeightKg) &&
                   (CurrentVolumeLiters + addedVolume <= MaxVolumeLiters);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Jobs\ItemDegradationJob.cs ===
using System;
using Unity.Burst;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Jobs;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    [BurstCompile]
    public struct ItemDegradationJob : IJob
    {
        public NativeList<InventoryHeaderData> Inventories;

        public NativeList<InventorySlotData> Slots;

        [ReadOnly] public NativeParallelHashMap<ushort, ItemPhysicalProperties> ItemProperties;

        public ulong CurrentTick;

        public byte AmbientMoisture;


        public void Execute()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < Slots.Length; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = Slots[i];

                if (!ItemProperties.TryGetValue(slot.ItemTypeId, out ItemPhysicalProperties properties))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                int headerIndex = FindHeaderIndex(slot.InventoryId);

                if (headerIndex < 0)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                InventoryHeaderData header = Inventories[headerIndex];

                ApplyMoistureDiffusion(ref slot, properties, header.ProtectionLevel);

                bool isSpoiled = ProcessQualityDegradation(ref slot, properties);

                if (isSpoiled)
                {
                    float lostMass = slot.Quantity * properties.MassPerUnitKg;

                    float lostVolume = slot.Quantity * properties.VolumePerUnitLiters;

                    header.CurrentWeightKg = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentWeightKg - lostMass);

                    header.CurrentVolumeLiters = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentVolumeLiters - lostVolume);

                    header.SlotCount--;

                    Inventories[headerIndex] = header;

                    Slots.RemoveAtSwapBack(i);

                    i--;
                }
                else
                {
                    Slots[i] = slot;
                }
            }
        }


        private int FindHeaderIndex(int inventoryId)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < Inventories.Length; i++)
            {
                if (Inventories[i].InventoryId == inventoryId)
                {
                    return i;
                }
            }

            return -1;
        }


        private void ApplyMoistureDiffusion(ref InventorySlotData slot, ItemPhysicalProperties properties, byte protectionLevel)
        {
            if (properties.MoistureAbsorbRate <= 0.0f || AmbientMoisture <= slot.Moisture)
            {
                return;
            }

            float exposureFactor = (255 - protectionLevel) / 255.0f;

            if (exposureFactor <= 0.01f)
            {
                return;
            }

            int moistureDiff = AmbientMoisture - slot.Moisture;

            float delta = moistureDiff * properties.MoistureAbsorbRate * exposureFactor;

            slot.Moisture = (byte)Math.Min(255, slot.Moisture + (int)Math.Max(1.0f, delta));
        }


        private bool ProcessQualityDegradation(ref InventorySlotData slot, ItemPhysicalProperties properties)
        {
            if (properties.ShelfLifeTicks == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            ulong ageTicks = CurrentTick > slot.CreationTick ? CurrentTick - slot.CreationTick : 0;

            float ageRatio = (float)ageTicks / properties.ShelfLifeTicks;

            float moistureMultiplier = 1.0f + (slot.Moisture / 255.0f) * 2.0f;

            int qualityLoss = (int)(ageRatio * 255.0f * moistureMultiplier);

            int currentCalculatedQuality = 255 - qualityLoss;

            if (currentCalculatedQuality <= 0)
            {
                slot.Quality = 0;

                return true;
            }

            slot.Quality = (byte)currentCalculatedQuality;

            return false;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Jobs\TransferRequest.cs ===
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    public enum TransferResultStatus : byte
    {
        Pending = 0,
        Success = 1,
        Partial = 2,
        FailedSourceEmpty = 3,
        FailedTargetFull = 4,
        FailedInvalidInventory = 5,
        FailedInvalidItem = 6
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct TransferRequest
    {
        public int SourceInventoryId;

        public int TargetInventoryId;

        public ushort ItemTypeId;

        public uint RequestedQuantity;

        public uint TransferredQuantity;

        public TransferResultStatus Status;


        public static TransferRequest Create(int sourceId, int targetId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantity)
        {
            return new TransferRequest
            {
                SourceInventoryId = sourceId,
                TargetInventoryId = targetId,
                ItemTypeId = itemTypeId,
                RequestedQuantity = quantity,
                TransferredQuantity = 0,
                Status = TransferResultStatus.Pending
            };
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Jobs\TransferResourceJob.cs ===
using System;
using Unity.Burst;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Jobs;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    [BurstCompile]
    public struct TransferResourceJob : IJob
    {
        public NativeList<InventoryHeaderData> Inventories;

        public NativeList<InventorySlotData> Slots;

        [ReadOnly] public NativeParallelHashMap<ushort, ItemPhysicalProperties> ItemProperties;

        public NativeArray<TransferRequest> Requests;

        public ulong CurrentTick;


        public void Execute()
        {
            for (int r = 0; r < Requests.Length; r++)
            {
                TransferRequest request = Requests[r];

                ProcessTransfer(ref request);

                Requests[r] = request;
            }
        }


        private void ProcessTransfer(ref TransferRequest request)
        {
            if (request.SourceInventoryId == request.TargetInventoryId || request.RequestedQuantity == 0)
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedInvalidInventory;
                return;
            }

            if (!ItemProperties.TryGetValue(request.ItemTypeId, out ItemPhysicalProperties properties))
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedInvalidItem;
                return;
            }

            int sourceHeaderIndex = FindHeaderIndex(request.SourceInventoryId);

            int targetHeaderIndex = FindHeaderIndex(request.TargetInventoryId);

            if (sourceHeaderIndex < 0 || targetHeaderIndex < 0)
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedInvalidInventory;
                return;
            }

            InventoryHeaderData sourceHeader = Inventories[sourceHeaderIndex];

            InventoryHeaderData targetHeader = Inventories[targetHeaderIndex];

            uint sourceAvailable = CalculateAvailableQuantity(request.SourceInventoryId, request.ItemTypeId);

            if (sourceAvailable == 0)
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedSourceEmpty;
                return;
            }

            uint maxAffordableByWeight = uint.MaxValue;

            if (properties.MassPerUnitKg > 0.0f)
            {
                float remainingWeight = targetHeader.MaxWeightKg - targetHeader.CurrentWeightKg;

                if (remainingWeight <= 0.0f)
                {
                    request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedTargetFull;
                    return;
                }

                maxAffordableByWeight = (uint)(remainingWeight / properties.MassPerUnitKg);
            }

            uint maxAffordableByVolume = uint.MaxValue;

            if (properties.VolumePerUnitLiters > 0.0f)
            {
                float remainingVolume = targetHeader.MaxVolumeLiters - targetHeader.CurrentVolumeLiters;

                if (remainingVolume <= 0.0f)
                {
                    request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedTargetFull;
                    return;
                }

                maxAffordableByVolume = (uint)(remainingVolume / properties.VolumePerUnitLiters);
            }

            uint allowedByTarget = Math.Min(maxAffordableByWeight, maxAffordableByVolume);

            if (allowedByTarget == 0)
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedTargetFull;
                return;
            }

            uint toTransfer = Math.Min(request.RequestedQuantity, Math.Min(sourceAvailable, allowedByTarget));

            if (toTransfer == 0)
            {
                request.Status = TransferResultStatus.FailedTargetFull;
                return;
            }

            DeductFromSource(request.SourceInventoryId, request.ItemTypeId, toTransfer, ref sourceHeader);

            AddToTarget(request.TargetInventoryId, request.ItemTypeId, toTransfer, properties, ref targetHeader);

            float totalMass = toTransfer * properties.MassPerUnitKg;

            float totalVolume = toTransfer * properties.VolumePerUnitLiters;

            sourceHeader.CurrentWeightKg -= totalMass;

            sourceHeader.CurrentVolumeLiters -= totalVolume;

            targetHeader.CurrentWeightKg += totalMass;

            targetHeader.CurrentVolumeLiters += totalVolume;

            Inventories[sourceHeaderIndex] = sourceHeader;

            Inventories[targetHeaderIndex] = targetHeader;

            request.TransferredQuantity = toTransfer;

            request.Status = toTransfer == request.RequestedQuantity ? TransferResultStatus.Success : TransferResultStatus.Partial;
        }


        private int FindHeaderIndex(int inventoryId)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < Inventories.Length; i++)
            {
                if (Inventories[i].InventoryId == inventoryId)
                {
                    return i;
                }
            }

            return -1;
        }


        private uint CalculateAvailableQuantity(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId)
        {
            uint total = 0;

            for (int i = 0; i < Slots.Length; i++)
            {
                if (Slots[i].InventoryId == inventoryId && Slots[i].ItemTypeId == itemTypeId)
                {
                    total += Slots[i].Quantity;
                }
            }

            return total;
        }


        private void DeductFromSource(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantityToDeduct, ref InventoryHeaderData header)
        {
            uint remaining = quantityToDeduct;

            for (int i = 0; i < Slots.Length && remaining > 0; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = Slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == itemTypeId)
                {
                    if (slot.Quantity <= remaining)
                    {
                        remaining -= slot.Quantity;

                        Slots.RemoveAtSwapBack(i);

                        header.SlotCount--;

                        i--;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        slot.Quantity -= remaining;

                        remaining = 0;

                        Slots[i] = slot;
                    }
                }
            }
        }


        private void AddToTarget(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantityToAdd, ItemPhysicalProperties properties, ref InventoryHeaderData header)
        {
            uint remaining = quantityToAdd;

            for (int i = 0; i < Slots.Length && remaining > 0; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = Slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == itemTypeId)
                {
                    uint availableInStack = properties.MaxStackSize > slot.Quantity ? properties.MaxStackSize - slot.Quantity : 0;

                    if (availableInStack > 0)
                    {
                        uint amount = Math.Min(availableInStack, remaining);

                        slot.Quantity += amount;

                        remaining -= amount;

                        Slots[i] = slot;
                    }
                }
            }

            while (remaining > 0)
            {
                uint stackAmount = Math.Min(remaining, properties.MaxStackSize);

                InventorySlotData newSlot = InventorySlotData.Create(
                    inventoryId,
                    itemTypeId,
                    stackAmount,
                    CurrentTick);

                Slots.Add(newSlot);

                header.SlotCount++;

                remaining -= stackAmount;
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Management\FoodPreservationManager.cs ===
using System;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.Simulation.Time;
using ER.World.Inventory;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    public class FoodPreservationManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static FoodPreservationManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Spoilage Rates (Daily % lost in warm seasons)")]
        [SerializeField] private float rawMeatSpoilRate = 0.25f;

        [SerializeField] private float berrySpoilRate = 0.15f;


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private InventoryManager inventoryManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;


        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (clockManager == null) clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;

            if (inventoryManager == null) inventoryManager = InventoryManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed += HandleDailyFoodDecay;
            }

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed -= HandleDailyFoodDecay;
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        private void HandleDailyFoodDecay(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || inventoryManager == null || polityManager == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (timeData.IsFreezing)
            {
                return;
            }

            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            for (int a = 0; a < anchors.Count; a++)
            {
                int storageId = anchors[a].StorageInventoryId;

                if (storageId <= 0) continue;

                uint rawMeat = inventoryManager.GetItemCount(storageId, 4);

                if (rawMeat > 0)
                {
                    uint spoiledMeat = (uint)Mathf.Ceil(rawMeat * rawMeatSpoilRate);

                    spoiledMeat = Math.Min(rawMeat, spoiledMeat);

                    inventoryManager.ExecuteTransfer(storageId, 0, 4, spoiledMeat, out _);

                    Debug.Log($"[FoodPreservationManager] Summer warmth spoiled {spoiledMeat}x raw meat in Settlement {anchors[a].AnchorId}!");
                }

                uint berries = inventoryManager.GetItemCount(storageId, 3);

                if (berries > 0)
                {
                    uint spoiledBerries = (uint)Mathf.Ceil(berries * berrySpoilRate);

                    spoiledBerries = Math.Min(berries, spoiledBerries);

                    inventoryManager.ExecuteTransfer(storageId, 0, 3, spoiledBerries, out _);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Management\InventoryManager.cs ===
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Jobs;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.Simulation.Time;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    public class InventoryManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static InventoryManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Configuration")]
        [SerializeField] private ItemDatabaseConfig itemDatabase;

        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;


        private NativeList<InventoryHeaderData> inventories;

        private NativeList<InventorySlotData> slots;

        private NativeParallelHashMap<ushort, ItemPhysicalProperties> itemPropertiesMap;

        private int nextInventoryId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public ItemDatabaseConfig ItemDatabase => itemDatabase;

        public NativeList<InventoryHeaderData> RawInventories => inventories;

        public NativeList<InventorySlotData> RawSlots => slots;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeStorage();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            if (clockManager == null)
            {
                clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;
            }

            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed += HandleDayPassed;
            }

            if (itemDatabase != null)
            {
                itemDatabase.BuildLookup();

                RebuildItemPropertiesMap();
            }
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnDayPassed -= HandleDayPassed;
            }

            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeStorage();
        }


        public void InitializeStorage()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            inventories = new NativeList<InventoryHeaderData>(2048, Allocator.Persistent);

            slots = new NativeList<InventorySlotData>(8192, Allocator.Persistent);

            itemPropertiesMap = new NativeParallelHashMap<ushort, ItemPhysicalProperties>(256, Allocator.Persistent);

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        public void DisposeStorage()
        {
            if (itemPropertiesMap.IsCreated)
            {
                itemPropertiesMap.Dispose();
            }

            if (slots.IsCreated)
            {
                slots.Dispose();
            }

            if (inventories.IsCreated)
            {
                inventories.Dispose();
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        public void RebuildItemPropertiesMap()
        {
            if (!itemPropertiesMap.IsCreated || itemDatabase == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            itemPropertiesMap.Clear();

            IReadOnlyList<ItemTypeConfig> types = itemDatabase.ItemTypes;

            for (int i = 0; i < types.Count; i++)
            {
                ItemTypeConfig config = types[i];

                ItemPhysicalProperties properties = new ItemPhysicalProperties
                {
                    MassPerUnitKg = config.MassPerUnitKg,
                    VolumePerUnitLiters = config.VolumePerUnitLiters,
                    MaxStackSize = config.MaxStackSize,
                    ShelfLifeTicks = config.ShelfLifeTicks,
                    BaseFlammability = config.BaseFlammability,
                    MoistureAbsorbRate = config.MoistureAbsorbRate
                };

                itemPropertiesMap.TryAdd(config.ItemTypeId, properties);
            }
        }


        private void HandleDayPassed(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            RunItemDegradation(120);
        }


        public void RunItemDegradation(byte ambientMoisture)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || slots.Length == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            ulong currentTick = clockManager != null ? clockManager.CurrentTime.TotalTicks : 0;

            ItemDegradationJob job = new ItemDegradationJob
            {
                Inventories = inventories,
                Slots = slots,
                ItemProperties = itemPropertiesMap,
                CurrentTick = currentTick,
                AmbientMoisture = ambientMoisture
            };

            job.Run();
        }


        public int CreateInventory(float maxWeightKg, float maxVolumeLiters, byte protectionLevel = 128)
        {
            if (!isInitialized)
            {
                return 0;
            }

            int id = nextInventoryId++;

            InventoryHeaderData header = InventoryHeaderData.Create(id, maxWeightKg, maxVolumeLiters, protectionLevel);

            inventories.Add(header);

            return id;
        }


        public bool TryGetHeader(int inventoryId, out InventoryHeaderData header)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < inventories.Length; i++)
            {
                if (inventories[i].InventoryId == inventoryId)
                {
                    header = inventories[i];

                    return true;
                }
            }

            header = default;

            return false;
        }


        public bool TrySetHeader(int inventoryId, InventoryHeaderData updatedHeader)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < inventories.Length; i++)
            {
                if (inventories[i].InventoryId == inventoryId)
                {
                    inventories[i] = updatedHeader;

                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }


        public bool TryAddItem(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantity, byte quality = 255, byte moisture = 0)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || quantity == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (!TryGetHeader(inventoryId, out InventoryHeaderData header))
            {
                return false;
            }

            float unitMass = 1.0f;

            float unitVolume = 1.0f;

            uint maxStack = 100;

            if (itemDatabase != null && itemDatabase.TryGetItemConfig(itemTypeId, out ItemTypeConfig config))
            {
                unitMass = config.MassPerUnitKg;

                unitVolume = config.VolumePerUnitLiters;

                maxStack = config.MaxStackSize;
            }
            else
            {
                switch (itemTypeId)
                {
                    case 1: unitMass = 2.2f; unitVolume = 3.0f; maxStack = 50; break;
                    case 2: unitMass = 1.8f; unitVolume = 1.2f; maxStack = 50; break;
                    case 3: unitMass = 0.08f; unitVolume = 0.1f; maxStack = 200; break;
                    case 4: unitMass = 0.65f; unitVolume = 0.8f; maxStack = 50; break;
                    case 5: unitMass = 1.2f; unitVolume = 1.5f; maxStack = 30; break;
                    case 6: unitMass = 0.8f; unitVolume = 1.2f; maxStack = 30; break;
                    case 7: unitMass = 1.5f; unitVolume = 3.0f; maxStack = 10; break;
                    case 8: unitMass = 0.5f; unitVolume = 0.6f; maxStack = 50; break;
                }
            }

            float addedWeight = unitMass * quantity;

            float addedVolume = unitVolume * quantity;

            if (!header.CanAccommodate(addedWeight, addedVolume))
            {
                return false;
            }

            ulong currentTick = clockManager != null ? clockManager.CurrentTime.TotalTicks : 0;

            uint remainingToAdd = quantity;

            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == itemTypeId && slot.Quality == quality)
                {
                    uint spaceInSlot = maxStack > slot.Quantity ? maxStack - slot.Quantity : 0;

                    if (spaceInSlot > 0)
                    {
                        uint amountToMerge = Math.Min(spaceInSlot, remainingToAdd);

                        slot.Quantity += amountToMerge;

                        remainingToAdd -= amountToMerge;

                        slots[i] = slot;

                        if (remainingToAdd == 0)
                        {
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            while (remainingToAdd > 0)
            {
                uint stackAmount = Math.Min(remainingToAdd, maxStack);

                InventorySlotData newSlot = InventorySlotData.Create(
                    inventoryId,
                    itemTypeId,
                    stackAmount,
                    currentTick,
                    quality,
                    moisture);

                slots.Add(newSlot);

                header.SlotCount++;

                remainingToAdd -= stackAmount;
            }

            header.CurrentWeightKg += addedWeight;

            header.CurrentVolumeLiters += addedVolume;

            TrySetHeader(inventoryId, header);

            return true;
        }


        public bool TryRemoveItem(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantity, out uint removedAmount)
        {
            removedAmount = 0;

            if (!isInitialized || quantity == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (!TryGetHeader(inventoryId, out InventoryHeaderData header))
            {
                return false;
            }

            uint remainingToRemove = quantity;

            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == itemTypeId)
                {
                    uint toTake = Math.Min(slot.Quantity, remainingToRemove);

                    slot.Quantity -= toTake;

                    remainingToRemove -= toTake;

                    removedAmount += toTake;

                    if (slot.Quantity == 0)
                    {
                        slots.RemoveAtSwapBack(i);

                        header.SlotCount--;

                        i--;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        slots[i] = slot;
                    }

                    if (remainingToRemove == 0)
                    {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (removedAmount > 0)
            {
                float unitMass = 1.0f;

                float unitVol = 1.0f;

                if (itemPropertiesMap.IsCreated && itemPropertiesMap.TryGetValue(itemTypeId, out ItemPhysicalProperties props))
                {
                    unitMass = props.MassPerUnitKg;

                    unitVol = props.VolumePerUnitLiters;
                }
                else if (itemDatabase != null && itemDatabase.TryGetItemConfig(itemTypeId, out ItemTypeConfig cfg))
                {
                    unitMass = cfg.MassPerUnitKg;

                    unitVol = cfg.VolumePerUnitLiters;
                }

                header.CurrentWeightKg = Mathf.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentWeightKg - (removedAmount * unitMass));

                header.CurrentVolumeLiters = Mathf.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentVolumeLiters - (removedAmount * unitVol));

                TrySetHeader(inventoryId, header);

                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }


        public TransferResultStatus ExecuteTransfer(int sourceId, int targetId, ushort itemTypeId, uint quantity, out uint transferredAmount)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || quantity == 0)
            {
                transferredAmount = 0;

                return TransferResultStatus.FailedInvalidInventory;
            }

            if (targetId == 0)
            {
                if (TryRemoveItem(sourceId, itemTypeId, quantity, out transferredAmount))
                {
                    return TransferResultStatus.Success;
                }

                return TransferResultStatus.FailedInvalidInventory;
            }

            NativeArray<TransferRequest> singleRequestArray = new NativeArray<TransferRequest>(1, Allocator.TempJob);

            singleRequestArray[0] = TransferRequest.Create(sourceId, targetId, itemTypeId, quantity);

            ulong currentTick = clockManager != null ? clockManager.CurrentTime.TotalTicks : 0;

            TransferResourceJob job = new TransferResourceJob
            {
                Inventories = inventories,
                Slots = slots,
                ItemProperties = itemPropertiesMap,
                Requests = singleRequestArray,
                CurrentTick = currentTick
            };

            job.Run();

            TransferRequest result = singleRequestArray[0];

            transferredAmount = result.TransferredQuantity;

            TransferResultStatus status = result.Status;

            singleRequestArray.Dispose();

            return status;
        }


        public uint GetItemCount(int inventoryId, ushort itemTypeId)
        {
            if (!isInitialized)
            {
                return 0;
            }

            uint total = 0;

            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                if (slots[i].InventoryId == inventoryId && slots[i].ItemTypeId == itemTypeId)
                {
                    total += slots[i].Quantity;
                }
            }

            return total;
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\04_Inventory\Management\ToolDurabilitySystem.cs ===
using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ER.World.Inventory
{
    public static class ToolDurabilitySystem
    {
        public static float GetToolEfficiency(InventoryManager inventoryManager, int inventoryId, ushort toolItemTypeId)
        {
            if (inventoryManager == null || !inventoryManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return 0.35f;
            }

            var slots = inventoryManager.RawSlots;

            byte highestQuality = 0;

            bool found = false;

            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == toolItemTypeId && slot.Quantity > 0)
                {
                    if (slot.Quality > highestQuality)
                    {
                        highestQuality = slot.Quality;

                        found = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!found)
            {
                return 0.35f;
            }

            float qualityRatio = highestQuality / 255.0f;

            return 0.40f + 0.60f * qualityRatio;
        }


        public static bool ApplyWear(InventoryManager inventoryManager, int inventoryId, ushort toolItemTypeId, byte wearAmount, out bool toolBroke)
        {
            toolBroke = false;

            if (inventoryManager == null || !inventoryManager.IsInitialized || wearAmount == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            var slots = inventoryManager.RawSlots;

            int targetSlotIndex = -1;

            byte lowestQuality = byte.MaxValue;

            for (int i = 0; i < slots.Length; i++)
            {
                InventorySlotData slot = slots[i];

                if (slot.InventoryId == inventoryId && slot.ItemTypeId == toolItemTypeId && slot.Quantity > 0)
                {
                    if (slot.Quality <= lowestQuality)
                    {
                        lowestQuality = slot.Quality;

                        targetSlotIndex = i;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (targetSlotIndex < 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            InventorySlotData targetSlot = slots[targetSlotIndex];

            if (targetSlot.Quality <= wearAmount)
            {
                toolBroke = true;

                if (!inventoryManager.ItemDatabase.TryGetItemConfig(toolItemTypeId, out ItemTypeConfig config))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                if (targetSlot.Quantity > 1)
                {
                    targetSlot.Quantity--;

                    targetSlot.Quality = 255;

                    slots[targetSlotIndex] = targetSlot;

                    if (inventoryManager.TryGetHeader(inventoryId, out InventoryHeaderData header))
                    {
                        header.CurrentWeightKg = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentWeightKg - config.MassPerUnitKg);

                        header.CurrentVolumeLiters = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentVolumeLiters - config.VolumePerUnitLiters);

                        inventoryManager.TrySetHeader(inventoryId, header);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (inventoryManager.TryGetHeader(inventoryId, out InventoryHeaderData header))
                    {
                        header.CurrentWeightKg = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentWeightKg - config.MassPerUnitKg);

                        header.CurrentVolumeLiters = Math.Max(0.0f, header.CurrentVolumeLiters - config.VolumePerUnitLiters);

                        header.SlotCount--;

                        inventoryManager.TrySetHeader(inventoryId, header);
                    }

                    slots.RemoveAtSwapBack(targetSlotIndex);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                targetSlot.Quality -= wearAmount;

                slots[targetSlotIndex] = targetSlot;
            }

            return true;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using ER.World.Command;

namespace ER.World.Command.Communication
{
    public enum CommunicationNodeType : byte
    {
        None = 0,
        CampHearth = 1,
        Watchtower = 2,
        OpticalRelayTower = 3,
        TelegraphSwitchboard = 4,
        CentralHQStation = 5
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct CommunicationNodeData
    {
        public ushort NodeId;

        public ushort PolityId;

        public ushort AnchorId;

        public CommunicationNodeType NodeType;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public float TransmissionRange;

        public bool IsActive;


        public CommunicationNodeType Type => NodeType;

        public float WorldX => WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldY => WorldPositionY;


        public static CommunicationNodeData Create(
            ushort id,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            CommunicationNodeType type,
            float worldX,
            float worldY,
            float range)
        {
            return new CommunicationNodeData
            {
                NodeId = id,
                PolityId = polityId,
                AnchorId = anchorId,
                NodeType = type,
                WorldPositionX = worldX,
                WorldPositionY = worldY,
                TransmissionRange = range,
                IsActive = true
            };
        }
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct CommunicationLineData
    {
        public int LineId;

        public ushort PolityId;

        public ushort NodeAId;

        public ushort NodeBId;

        public TransmissionMedium Medium;

        public float Length;

        public bool IsActive;


        public bool IsFunctional => IsActive;


        public static CommunicationLineData Create(
            int id,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort nodeAId,
            ushort nodeBId,
            TransmissionMedium medium,
            float length)
        {
            return new CommunicationLineData
            {
                LineId = id,
                PolityId = polityId,
                NodeAId = nodeAId,
                NodeBId = nodeBId,
                Medium = medium,
                Length = length,
                IsActive = true
            };
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\05_Command\Communication\CommunicationNetworkManager.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Polity;
using ER.World.Command;

namespace ER.World.Command.Communication
{
    public class CommunicationNetworkManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static CommunicationNetworkManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("References")]
        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;

        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;


        [Header("Gizmo Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool drawNetworkGizmos = false;


        private readonly List<CommunicationNodeData> nodes = new List<CommunicationNodeData>();

        private readonly List<CommunicationLineData> lines = new List<CommunicationLineData>();

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, CommunicationNodeData> nodeLookup = new Dictionary<ushort, CommunicationNodeData>();

        private readonly Dictionary<ushort, List<ushort>> nodeConnectedLines = new Dictionary<ushort, List<ushort>>();

        private ushort nextNodeId = 1;

        private ushort nextLineId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public IReadOnlyList<CommunicationNodeData> Nodes => nodes;

        public IReadOnlyList<CommunicationLineData> Lines => lines;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureNetworkInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureNetworkInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager != null)
            {
                polityManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (polityManager == null || !polityManager.IsInitialized || polityManager.Anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            nodes.Clear();

            lines.Clear();

            nodeLookup.Clear();

            nodeConnectedLines.Clear();

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            for (int i = 0; i < anchors.Count; i++)
            {
                var anchor = anchors[i];

                float hqX = (anchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float hqY = (anchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                RegisterNode(anchor.OwnerPolityId, anchor.AnchorId, CommunicationNodeType.CampHearth, hqX, hqY, 25.0f);
            }

            isInitialized = true;

            Debug.Log($"[CommunicationNetworkManager] Initialized {nodes.Count} primitive tribal hearth command points.");
        }


        public ushort RegisterNode(
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            CommunicationNodeType type,
            float worldX,
            float worldY,
            float range)
        {
            ushort id = nextNodeId++;

            CommunicationNodeData node = CommunicationNodeData.Create(id, polityId, anchorId, type, worldX, worldY, range);

            nodes.Add(node);

            nodeLookup.Add(id, node);

            nodeConnectedLines.Add(id, new List<ushort>());

            return id;
        }


        public ushort ConnectNodes(
            ushort polityId,
            ushort nodeAId,
            ushort nodeBId,
            TransmissionMedium medium)
        {
            if (!nodeLookup.TryGetValue(nodeAId, out CommunicationNodeData nodeA) ||
                !nodeLookup.TryGetValue(nodeBId, out CommunicationNodeData nodeB))
            {
                return 0;
            }

            ushort id = nextLineId++;

            float length = Vector2.Distance(
                new Vector2(nodeA.WorldPositionX, nodeA.WorldPositionY),
                new Vector2(nodeB.WorldPositionX, nodeB.WorldPositionY));

            CommunicationLineData line = CommunicationLineData.Create(id, polityId, nodeAId, nodeBId, medium, length);

            lines.Add(line);

            nodeConnectedLines[nodeAId].Add(id);

            nodeConnectedLines[nodeBId].Add(id);

            return id;
        }


        public bool TryFindFastestPath(
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            Vector2 targetPos,
            out TransmissionMedium networkMedium,
            out float remainingCourierDistance)
        {
            networkMedium = TransmissionMedium.AcousticVoice;

            remainingCourierDistance = float.MaxValue;

            if (!isInitialized || lines.Count == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            return false;
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!drawNetworkGizmos || !isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < nodes.Count; i++)
            {
                CommunicationNodeData node = nodes[i];

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(node.WorldPositionX, node.WorldPositionY, -2.6f);

                Gizmos.color = Color.green;

                Gizmos.DrawWireSphere(pos, node.TransmissionRange);
            }
        }
    }
}

namespace ER.World.Command
