    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct GameCommand
    {
        public ulong CommandId;

        public CommandType Type;

        public ushort SourcePolityId;

        public ushort SourceAnchorId;

        public float TargetWorldX;

        public float TargetWorldY;

        public int TargetEntityId;

        public ushort PayloadId;

        public uint PayloadQuantity;

        public ulong IssuedTick;

        public ulong DeliveryTick;

        public CommandStatus Status;

        public TransmissionMedium Medium;


        public static GameCommand Create(
            ulong id,
            CommandType type,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            float targetX,
            float targetY,
            ulong issuedTick,
            ulong deliveryTick,
            TransmissionMedium medium,
            int targetEntity = -1,
            ushort payloadId = 0,
            uint payloadQuantity = 0)
        {
            return new GameCommand
            {
                CommandId = id,
                Type = type,
                SourcePolityId = polityId,
                SourceAnchorId = anchorId,
                TargetWorldX = targetX,
                TargetWorldY = targetY,
                TargetEntityId = targetEntity,
                PayloadId = payloadId,
                PayloadQuantity = payloadQuantity,
                IssuedTick = issuedTick,
                DeliveryTick = deliveryTick,
                Status = CommandStatus.QueuedInTransit,
                Medium = medium
            };
        }
    }

using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Population;
using ER.World.Population.Visual;
using ER.World.Construction;
using ER.World.Inventory;
using ER.World.Polity;

namespace ER.World.Command.Interaction
{
    public class StrategicInteractionManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static StrategicInteractionManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Selection Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private Color selectionRingColor = new Color(0.2f, 0.9f, 1.0f, 0.9f);

        [SerializeField] private float ringRadius = 1.35f;


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private PopulationManager populationManager;

        [SerializeField] private ConstructionManager constructionManager;

        [SerializeField] private InventoryManager inventoryManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;


        private Camera mainCamera;

        private int selectedPopId = -1;

        private int selectedPopIndex = -1;

        private bool isBuildMode = false;

        private BuildingType activeBuildType = BuildingType.DugoutShelter;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public int SelectedPopId => selectedPopId;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            mainCamera = Camera.main;

            if (populationManager == null) populationManager = PopulationManager.Instance;

            if (constructionManager == null) constructionManager = ConstructionManager.Instance;

            if (inventoryManager == null) inventoryManager = InventoryManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void Update()
        {
            if (!isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (mainCamera == null)
            {
                mainCamera = Camera.main;

                if (mainCamera == null) return;
            }

            HandleInputHotkeys();

            HandleMouseClicks();
        }


        private void HandleInputHotkeys()
        {
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.B))
            {
                isBuildMode = !isBuildMode || activeBuildType != BuildingType.DugoutShelter;

                activeBuildType = BuildingType.DugoutShelter;
            }

            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.R))
            {
                isBuildMode = !isBuildMode || activeBuildType != BuildingType.DryingRack;

                activeBuildType = BuildingType.DryingRack;
            }

            if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
            {
                isBuildMode = false;

                selectedPopId = -1;

                selectedPopIndex = -1;
            }
        }


        private void HandleMouseClicks()
        {
            if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
            {
                Vector3 mouseWorld = mainCamera.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);

                Vector2 clickPoint = new Vector2(mouseWorld.x, mouseWorld.y);

                if (isBuildMode)
                {
                    PlaceCurrentBlueprint(clickPoint);

                    return;
                }

                Collider2D hit = Physics2D.OverlapPoint(clickPoint);

                if (hit != null && hit.TryGetComponent<AgentSelectable>(out AgentSelectable selectable))
                {
                    selectedPopId = selectable.PopId;

                    selectedPopIndex = selectable.PopIndex;
                }
                else
                {
                    selectedPopId = -1;

                    selectedPopIndex = -1;
                }
            }

            if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(1) && isBuildMode)
            {
                isBuildMode = false;
            }
        }


        private void PlaceCurrentBlueprint(Vector2 worldPos)
        {
            if (constructionManager == null || polityManager == null || polityManager.Anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            var playerAnchor = polityManager.Anchors[0];

            constructionManager.PlaceBlueprint(activeBuildType, worldPos.x, worldPos.y, playerAnchor.OwnerPolityId, playerAnchor.AnchorId);

            if (!Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift))
            {
                isBuildMode = false;
            }
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (selectedPopId > 0 && populationManager != null && populationManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                NativeArray<PopData> pops = populationManager.ActivePops;

                if (pops.IsCreated && selectedPopIndex >= 0 && selectedPopIndex < pops.Length)
                {
                    PopData pop = pops[selectedPopIndex];

                    if (pop.IsAlive && pop.PopId == selectedPopId)
                    {
                        Vector3 pos = new Vector3(pop.WorldPositionX, pop.WorldPositionY, -3.15f);

                        Gizmos.color = selectionRingColor;

                        Gizmos.DrawWireSphere(pos, ringRadius);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (isBuildMode && mainCamera != null)
            {
                Vector3 mouseWorld = mainCamera.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(mouseWorld.x, mouseWorld.y, -2.5f);

                Gizmos.color = new Color(0.2f, 1.0f, 0.4f, 0.8f);

                Gizmos.DrawWireCube(pos, new Vector3(2.8f, 2.8f, 1.6f));
            }
        }


        private void OnGUI()
        {
            DrawControlsHint();

            DrawSettlementStorageBar();

            if (selectedPopId > 0 && populationManager != null && populationManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                DrawSelectedPopCard();
            }
        }

=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\05_Command\Jobs\CommandDispatchJob.cs ===
using Unity.Burst;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Jobs;

namespace ER.World.Command
{
    [BurstCompile]
    public struct CommandDispatchJob : IJob
    {
        public NativeList<GameCommand> ActiveCommands;

        public NativeQueue<GameCommand>.ParallelWriter ReadyCommandsQueue;

        public ulong CurrentTick;


        public void Execute()
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < ActiveCommands.Length; i++)
            {
                GameCommand cmd = ActiveCommands[i];

                if (cmd.Status == CommandStatus.QueuedInTransit)
                {
                    if (CurrentTick >= cmd.DeliveryTick)
                    {
                        cmd.Status = CommandStatus.Delivered;

                        ActiveCommands[i] = cmd;

                        ReadyCommandsQueue.Enqueue(cmd);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\05_Command\Management\CommandQueueManager.cs ===
using System;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Jobs;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.Simulation.Time;
using ER.World.Polity;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Command.Communication;
using ER.World.Navigation;

namespace ER.World.Command
{
    public class CommandQueueManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static CommandQueueManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Physical Transmission Velocities (World Units per Tick)")]
        [SerializeField] private float acousticRangeUnits = 18.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float runnerSpeedPerTick = 0.35f;

        [SerializeField] private float horseSpeedPerTick = 1.10f;

        [SerializeField] private float heliographSpeedPerTick = 25.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float mountedCourierThresholdDistance = 40.0f;


        [Header("References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;

        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private CommunicationNetworkManager communicationNetwork;

        [SerializeField] private MacroPathfindingManager macroNavManager;


        private NativeList<GameCommand> activeCommands;

        private NativeQueue<GameCommand> deliveredCommandsQueue;

        private ulong nextCommandId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public event Action<GameCommand> OnCommandDelivered;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public int ActiveCommandsInTransitCount => activeCommands.IsCreated ? activeCommands.Length : 0;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeQueues();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (clockManager == null) clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (communicationNetwork == null) communicationNetwork = CommunicationNetworkManager.Instance;

            if (macroNavManager == null) macroNavManager = MacroPathfindingManager.Instance;

            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnTick += ProcessCommandQueueOnTick;
            }

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (clockManager != null)
            {
                clockManager.OnTick -= ProcessCommandQueueOnTick;
            }

            DisposeQueues();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeQueues();
        }


        public void InitializeQueues()
        {
            if (activeCommands.IsCreated && deliveredCommandsQueue.IsCreated)
            {
                return;
            }

            activeCommands = new NativeList<GameCommand>(1024, Allocator.Persistent);

            deliveredCommandsQueue = new NativeQueue<GameCommand>(Allocator.Persistent);
        }


        public void DisposeQueues()
        {
            if (activeCommands.IsCreated)
            {
                activeCommands.Dispose();
            }

            if (deliveredCommandsQueue.IsCreated)
            {
                deliveredCommandsQueue.Dispose();
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        public ulong IssueCommand(
            CommandType type,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            float targetWorldX,
            float targetWorldY,
            int targetEntity = -1,
            ushort payloadId = 0,
            uint payloadQuantity = 0)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || !ValidateWorldTargetPlacement(targetWorldX, targetWorldY))
            {
                return 0;
            }

            ulong currentTick = clockManager != null ? clockManager.CurrentTime.TotalTicks : 0;

            Vector2 sourcePos = ResolveAnchorWorldPosition(anchorId);

            Vector2 targetPos = new Vector2(targetWorldX, targetWorldY);

            float totalDistance = Vector2.Distance(sourcePos, targetPos);

            ulong delayTicks = CalculateTransmissionDelay(polityId, anchorId, sourcePos, targetPos, totalDistance, out TransmissionMedium medium);

            ulong deliveryTick = currentTick + delayTicks;

            ulong commandId = nextCommandId++;

            GameCommand command = GameCommand.Create(
                commandId,
                type,
                polityId,
                anchorId,
                targetWorldX,
                targetWorldY,
                currentTick,
                deliveryTick,
                medium,
                targetEntity,
                payloadId,
                payloadQuantity);

            activeCommands.Add(command);

            return commandId;
        }


        public bool ValidateWorldTargetPlacement(float worldX, float worldY)
        {
            if (gridManager == null || !gridManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                return false;
            }

            WorldMapConfig config = gridManager.MapConfig;

            int tileX = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldX / config.TileWorldSize);

            int tileY = Mathf.FloorToInt(worldY / config.TileWorldSize);

            if (!config.IsCoordsValid(tileX, tileY))
            {
                return false;
            }

            TileData tile = gridManager.GetTile(tileX, tileY);

            if (tile.Terrain == TerrainType.WaterDeep)
            {
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }


        private Vector2 ResolveAnchorWorldPosition(ushort anchorId)
        {
            if (polityManager == null || gridManager == null)
            {
                return Vector2.zero;
            }

            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            for (int i = 0; i < anchors.Count; i++)
            {
                if (anchors[i].AnchorId == anchorId)
                {
                    return new Vector2((anchors[i].TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize, (anchors[i].TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize);
                }
            }

            return Vector2.zero;
        }


        private ulong CalculateTransmissionDelay(
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            Vector2 sourcePos,
            Vector2 targetPos,
            float directDistance,
            out TransmissionMedium usedMedium)
        {
            if (directDistance <= acousticRangeUnits)
            {
                usedMedium = TransmissionMedium.AcousticVoice;

                return 0;
            }

            float physicalTravelDistance = directDistance;

            if (macroNavManager == null)
            {
                macroNavManager = MacroPathfindingManager.Instance;
            }

            if (macroNavManager != null && macroNavManager.IsInitialized)
            {
                var macroPath = macroNavManager.FindMacroPathBetweenPositions(sourcePos, targetPos);

                if (macroPath.Success && macroPath.TotalDistanceMeters > 1.0f)
                {
                    physicalTravelDistance = macroPath.TotalDistanceMeters;
                }
            }

            if (communicationNetwork != null && communicationNetwork.TryFindFastestPath(polityId, anchorId, targetPos, out TransmissionMedium networkMedium, out float remainingCourierDistance))
            {
                usedMedium = networkMedium;

                ulong networkDelay = networkMedium == TransmissionMedium.ElectricTelegraph ? 1UL : (ulong)Mathf.Max(1.0f, physicalTravelDistance / heliographSpeedPerTick);

                float courierSpeed = remainingCourierDistance > mountedCourierThresholdDistance ? horseSpeedPerTick : runnerSpeedPerTick;

                ulong courierDelay = (ulong)Mathf.Max(0.0f, remainingCourierDistance / Mathf.Max(0.01f, courierSpeed));

                return networkDelay + courierDelay;
            }

            if (physicalTravelDistance > mountedCourierThresholdDistance)
            {
                usedMedium = TransmissionMedium.MountedMessenger;

                return (ulong)Mathf.Max(1.0f, physicalTravelDistance / Mathf.Max(0.01f, horseSpeedPerTick));
            }

            usedMedium = TransmissionMedium.RunnerCourier;

            return (ulong)Mathf.Max(1.0f, physicalTravelDistance / Mathf.Max(0.01f, runnerSpeedPerTick));
        }


        private void ProcessCommandQueueOnTick(WorldTimeData timeData)
        {
            if (!isInitialized || activeCommands.Length == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            CommandDispatchJob job = new CommandDispatchJob
            {
                ActiveCommands = activeCommands,
                ReadyCommandsQueue = deliveredCommandsQueue.AsParallelWriter(),
                CurrentTick = timeData.TotalTicks
            };

            JobHandle handle = job.Schedule();

            handle.Complete();

            while (deliveredCommandsQueue.TryDequeue(out GameCommand deliveredCmd))
            {
                RemoveCompletedCommandFromList(deliveredCmd.CommandId);

                OnCommandDelivered?.Invoke(deliveredCmd);
            }
        }


        private void RemoveCompletedCommandFromList(ulong commandId)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < activeCommands.Length; i++)
            {
                if (activeCommands[i].CommandId == commandId)
                {
                    activeCommands.RemoveAtSwapBack(i);

                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        [Header("Reproduction & Matrimony")]
        [SerializeField] private ushort pregnancyDurationDays = 48;

        [SerializeField] private ushort postPartumCooldownDays = 16;

        [SerializeField] private float dailyConceptionProbability = 0.04f;

        [SerializeField] private float conceptionMinHealth = 180.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float conceptionMinCalories = 1200.0f;

        [SerializeField] private float conceptionMinBodyTemp = 35.5f;


        public ushort InfantMaxDays => infantMaxDays;

        public ushort ChildMaxDays => childMaxDays;

        public ushort YouthMaxDays => youthMaxDays;

        public ushort AdultMaxDays => adultMaxDays;

        public ushort ElderBaseMortalityAgeDays => elderBaseMortalityAgeDays;

        public ushort StartingAdultMinAgeDays => startingAdultMinAgeDays;

        public ushort StartingAdultMaxAgeDays => startingAdultMaxAgeDays;

        public float BaseSenescenceDailyChance => baseSenescenceDailyChance;

        public float SenescenceAgeCurveMultiplier => senescenceAgeCurveMultiplier;

        public ushort PregnancyDurationDays => pregnancyDurationDays;

        public ushort PostPartumCooldownDays => postPartumCooldownDays;

        public float DailyConceptionProbability => dailyConceptionProbability;

        public float ConceptionMinHealth => conceptionMinHealth;

        public float ConceptionMinCalories => conceptionMinCalories;

        public float ConceptionMinBodyTemp => conceptionMinBodyTemp;


        public PopLifeStage ResolveLifeStage(ushort ageInDays)
        {
            if (ageInDays < infantMaxDays) return PopLifeStage.Infant;

            if (ageInDays < childMaxDays) return PopLifeStage.Child;

            if (ageInDays < youthMaxDays) return PopLifeStage.Youth;

            if (ageInDays < adultMaxDays) return PopLifeStage.Adult;

            return PopLifeStage.Elder;
        }
    }

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Crafting
{
    public enum CraftingStationType : byte
    {
        None = 0,
        CampHearth = 1,
        TanningRack = 2,
        FlintKnappingStation = 3,
        CarpentryBench = 4,
        DryingRack = 5
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct CraftingStationData
    {
        public int StationId;

        public ushort PolityId;

        public ushort HomeAnchorId;

        public CraftingStationType StationType;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public bool IsOccupied;

        public bool IsActive;


        public static CraftingStationData Create(
            int id,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            CraftingStationType type,
            float x,
            float y)
        {
            return new CraftingStationData
            {
                StationId = id,
                PolityId = polityId,
                HomeAnchorId = anchorId,
                StationType = type,
                WorldPositionX = x,
                WorldPositionY = y,
                IsOccupied = false,
                IsActive = true
            };
        }
    }

    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct CraftingRecipeData
    {
        public ushort RecipeId;

        public CraftingStationType RequiredStation;

        public ushort InputItemA;

        public uint InputCountA;

        public ushort InputItemB;

        public uint InputCountB;

        public ushort OutputItem;

        public uint OutputCount;

        public float WorkLaborRequired;


        public static CraftingRecipeData Create(
            ushort recipeId,
            CraftingStationType station,
            ushort inA,
            uint countA,
            ushort outItem,
            uint outCount,
            float labor,
            ushort inB = 0,
            uint countB = 0)
        {
            return new CraftingRecipeData
            {
                RecipeId = recipeId,
                RequiredStation = station,
                InputItemA = inA,
                InputCountA = countA,
                InputItemB = inB,
                InputCountB = countB,
                OutputItem = outItem,
                OutputCount = outCount,
                WorkLaborRequired = labor
            };
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Unity\Evolution & Revolution\Assets\_Project\Scripts\06_Population\Crafting\CraftingManager.cs ===
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Mathematics;
using UnityEngine;
using ER.World.Grid;
using ER.World.Polity;
using ER.World.Inventory;
using ER.Simulation.Time;

namespace ER.World.Crafting
{
    public class CraftingManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static CraftingManager Instance { get; private set; }


        [Header("Starting Crafting Infrastructure")]
        [SerializeField] private int tanningRacksPerCamp = 3;

        [SerializeField] private int knappingStationsPerCamp = 2;

        [SerializeField] private float stationPlacementRadius = 15.0f;


        [Header("System References")]
        [SerializeField] private SimulationClockManager clockManager;

        [SerializeField] private PolityRegionManager polityManager;

        [SerializeField] private WorldGridManager gridManager;

        [SerializeField] private InventoryManager inventoryManager;


        [Header("Debug Visualizer")]
        [SerializeField] private bool drawStationGizmos = true;


        private NativeList<CraftingStationData> stations;

        private readonly List<CraftingRecipeData> recipes = new List<CraftingRecipeData>();

        private int nextStationId = 1;

        private bool isInitialized = false;


        public bool IsInitialized => isInitialized;

        public NativeList<CraftingStationData> RawStations => stations;


        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }

            Instance = this;

            InitializeStorage();

            RegisterDefaultRecipes();
        }


        private void Start()
        {
            EnsureInitialized();
        }


        public void EnsureInitialized()
        {
            if (isInitialized)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (clockManager == null) clockManager = SimulationClockManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager == null) polityManager = PolityRegionManager.Instance;

            if (gridManager == null) gridManager = WorldGridManager.Instance;

            if (inventoryManager == null) inventoryManager = InventoryManager.Instance;

            if (polityManager != null)
            {
                polityManager.EnsureInitialized();
            }

            if (polityManager == null || !polityManager.IsInitialized || polityManager.Anchors.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            SpawnSettlementStations();

            isInitialized = true;
        }


        private void InitializeStorage()
        {
            if (!stations.IsCreated)
            {
                stations = new NativeList<CraftingStationData>(256, Allocator.Persistent);
            }
        }


        private void RegisterDefaultRecipes()
        {
            recipes.Clear();

            recipes.Add(CraftingRecipeData.Create(1, CraftingStationType.TanningRack, 5, 2, 6, 2, 10.0f));

            recipes.Add(CraftingRecipeData.Create(2, CraftingStationType.TanningRack, 6, 2, 7, 1, 15.0f));

            recipes.Add(CraftingRecipeData.Create(3, CraftingStationType.DryingRack, 4, 2, 8, 2, 12.0f, 1, 1));
        }


        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            DisposeStorage();
        }


        private void DisposeStorage()
        {
            if (stations.IsCreated)
            {
                stations.Dispose();
            }

            isInitialized = false;
        }


        private void SpawnSettlementStations()
        {
            stations.Clear();

            var anchors = polityManager.Anchors;

            float tileSize = gridManager.MapConfig.TileWorldSize;

            for (int a = 0; a < anchors.Count; a++)
            {
                PolityAnchorData anchor = anchors[a];

                float centerX = (anchor.TileX + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                float centerY = (anchor.TileY + 0.5f) * tileSize;

                for (int t = 0; t < tanningRacksPerCamp; t++)
                {
                    float angle = (t / (float)tanningRacksPerCamp) * Mathf.PI + 0.35f;

                    float sx = centerX + Mathf.Cos(angle) * stationPlacementRadius;

                    float sy = centerY + Mathf.Sin(angle) * stationPlacementRadius;

                    RegisterStation(anchor.OwnerPolityId, anchor.AnchorId, CraftingStationType.TanningRack, sx, sy);
                }

                for (int k = 0; k < knappingStationsPerCamp; k++)
                {
                    float angle = (k / (float)knappingStationsPerCamp) * Mathf.PI + Mathf.PI + 0.35f;

                    float sx = centerX + Mathf.Cos(angle) * (stationPlacementRadius * 0.8f);

                    float sy = centerY + Mathf.Sin(angle) * (stationPlacementRadius * 0.8f);

                    RegisterStation(anchor.OwnerPolityId, anchor.AnchorId, CraftingStationType.FlintKnappingStation, sx, sy);
                }

                float dryX = centerX + stationPlacementRadius * 0.9f;

                float dryY = centerY - stationPlacementRadius * 0.6f;

                RegisterStation(anchor.OwnerPolityId, anchor.AnchorId, CraftingStationType.DryingRack, dryX, dryY);
            }

            Debug.Log($"[CraftingManager] Established {stations.Length} physical crafting stations.");
        }


        public int RegisterStation(ushort polityId, ushort anchorId, CraftingStationType type, float x, float y)
        {
            int id = nextStationId++;

            CraftingStationData station = CraftingStationData.Create(id, polityId, anchorId, type, x, y);

            stations.Add(station);

            return id;
        }


        public bool HasStationAvailable(ushort anchorId, CraftingStationType type)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < stations.Length; i++)
            {
                if (stations[i].HomeAnchorId == anchorId && stations[i].StationType == type && stations[i].IsActive)
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }


        public bool TryGetNearestAvailableStation(
            float2 popPos,
            ushort anchorId,
            CraftingStationType type,
            System.Func<int, bool> isStationBusyFunc,
            out int stationId,
            out float2 stationPos)
        {
            float minDist = float.MaxValue;

            stationPos = float2.zero;

            stationId = -1;

            bool found = false;

            for (int i = 0; i < stations.Length; i++)
            {
                CraftingStationData st = stations[i];

                if (st.HomeAnchorId == anchorId && st.StationType == type && st.IsActive)
                {
                    if (isStationBusyFunc != null && isStationBusyFunc(st.StationId))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    float dist = math.distance(popPos, new float2(st.WorldPositionX, st.WorldPositionY));

                    if (dist < minDist)
                    {
                        minDist = dist;

                        stationPos = new float2(st.WorldPositionX, st.WorldPositionY);

                        stationId = st.StationId;

                        found = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            return found;
        }


        private void OnDrawGizmos()
        {
            if (!drawStationGizmos || !stations.IsCreated || stations.Length == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < stations.Length; i++)
            {
                CraftingStationData s = stations[i];

                if (!s.IsActive) continue;

                Vector3 pos = new Vector3(s.WorldPositionX, s.WorldPositionY, -2.2f);

                if (s.StationType == CraftingStationType.TanningRack)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.85f, 0.5f, 0.2f);

                    Gizmos.DrawCube(pos, new Vector3(1.8f, 0.4f, 1.2f));
                }
                else if (s.StationType == CraftingStationType.FlintKnappingStation)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.45f, 0.55f, 0.65f);

                    Gizmos.DrawSphere(pos, 0.75f);
                }
                else if (s.StationType == CraftingStationType.DryingRack)
                {
                    Gizmos.color = new Color(0.7f, 0.25f, 0.15f);

                    Gizmos.DrawCube(pos, new Vector3(2.2f, 0.6f, 1.8f));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Population
{
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct PopData
    {
        public int PopId;

        public ushort PolityId;

        public ushort HomeAnchorId;

        public int PersonalInventoryId;

        public int HouseholdId;

        public int DwellingId;

        public float WorldPositionX;

        public float WorldPositionY;

        public float TargetPositionX;

        public float TargetPositionY;

        public float VelocityX;

        public float VelocityY;

        public PopActivityState ActivityState;

        public PopLifeStage LifeStage;

        public Gender Gender;

        public bool IsAlive;

        public bool IsBuried;

        public ulong BirthTick;

        public ushort AgeInDays;

        public byte Health;

        public float CurrentCalories;

        public float MaxCalories;

        public float CurrentHydration;

        public float BodyTemperature;

        public byte WarmthInsulation;

        public bool IsPregnant;

        public ushort PregnancyDays;

        public ushort MatingCooldownDays;

        public int SpousePopId;


        public static PopData Create(
            int popId,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort homeAnchorId,
            int inventoryId,
            float worldX,
            float worldY,
            ulong currentTick,
            ushort ageDays,
            Gender gender,
            PopDemographicsConfig config = null)
        {
            PopLifeStage stage = PopLifeStage.Adult;

            if (config != null)
            {
                stage = config.ResolveLifeStage(ageDays);
            }

            return new PopData
            {
                PopId = popId,
                PolityId = polityId,
                HomeAnchorId = homeAnchorId,
                PersonalInventoryId = inventoryId,
                HouseholdId = 0,
                DwellingId = 0,
                WorldPositionX = worldX,
                WorldPositionY = worldY,
                TargetPositionX = worldX,
                TargetPositionY = worldY,
                VelocityX = 0.0f,
                VelocityY = 0.0f,
                ActivityState = PopActivityState.Idle,
                LifeStage = stage,
                Gender = gender,
                IsAlive = true,
                IsBuried = false,
                BirthTick = currentTick,
                AgeInDays = ageDays,
                Health = 255,
                CurrentCalories = 2000.0f,
                MaxCalories = 2500.0f,
                CurrentHydration = 100.0f,
                BodyTemperature = 36.6f,
                WarmthInsulation = 20,
                IsPregnant = false,
                PregnancyDays = 0,
                MatingCooldownDays = 0,
                SpousePopId = 0
            };
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace ER.World.Housing
{
    [Serializable]
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
    public struct HouseholdData
    {
        public int HouseholdId;

        public ushort PolityId;

        public ushort HomeAnchorId;

        public int DwellingId;

        public int HeadPopId;

        public int SpousePopId;

        public byte ChildrenCount;

        public bool IsActive;


        public int TotalMembers => (HeadPopId > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (SpousePopId > 0 ? 1 : 0) + ChildrenCount;


        public static HouseholdData Create(
            int householdId,
            ushort polityId,
            ushort anchorId,
            int dwellingId,
            int headPopId,
            int spousePopId)
        {
            return new HouseholdData
            {
                HouseholdId = householdId,
                PolityId = polityId,
                HomeAnchorId = anchorId,
                DwellingId = dwellingId,
                HeadPopId = headPopId,
                SpousePopId = spousePopId,
                ChildrenCount = 0,
                IsActive = true
            };
        }
    }
